Perfect! Now let me create a comprehensive summary document for you. Here's the complete exploration of the Sandcastle Go CLI:

## Sandcastle CLI - Complete Architecture Overview

### 1. Directory Structure

```
/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/vendor/sandcastle-cli/
├── main.go                   # Entry point
├── go.mod                    # Go module definition
├── go.sum                    # Dependency checksums
├── Makefile                  # Build configuration
├── sandcastle                # Compiled binary
├── cmd/                      # Command implementations
│   ├── root.go              # Root command setup
│   ├── sandbox.go           # Create, list, delete, start, stop, set, rename, unarchive
│   ├── route.go             # Route add, list, delete
│   ├── status.go            # System status
│   ├── info.go              # Server information
│   ├── login.go             # Authentication via device flow
│   ├── config.go            # Config and server management
│   ├── connect.go           # SSH/mosh connection utilities
│   ├── exec.go              # Execute single command
│   ├── cp.go                # File copy via scp
│   ├── services.go          # Docker and VNC service management
│   ├── snapshot.go          # Snapshot creation and restoration
│   ├── token.go             # API token management
│   ├── users.go             # User administration
│   ├── tailscale.go         # Tailscale integration
│   └── smb.go               # SMB file sharing
├── api/                      # API client
│   ├── client.go            # HTTP client and API methods
│   └── types.go             # Request/response types
└── internal/
    └── config/
        └── config.go        # Configuration loading/saving
```

### 2. Dependencies (go.mod)

```
Module: github.com/sandcastle/cli
Go Version: 1.25.7

Direct Dependencies:
- github.com/spf13/cobra v1.10.2      # CLI framework
- golang.org/x/term v0.31.0           # Terminal utilities
- gopkg.in/yaml.v3 v3.0.1             # YAML config parsing

Indirect Dependencies:
- github.com/inconshreveable/mousetrap v1.1.0
- github.com/spf13/pflag v1.0.10
- golang.org/x/sys v0.32.0
```

### 3. Root Command Setup (cmd/root.go)

The root command is configured with:
- **Use**: `sandcastle`
- **Short**: "Sandcastle — shared Docker sandbox platform"
- **Long**: "CLI for managing Sandcastle development sandboxes."
- **Flags**: `SilenceUsage=true`, `SilenceErrors=true`
- **Version**: Set from build flags (Version, Commit, Date)
- **Initialize**: Adds `version` subcommand

Entry point: `Execute()` function calls `rootCmd.Execute()`

Helper function `printServer(client *api.Client)` displays active server alias and URL.

### 4. API Client Architecture (api/client.go)

#### Client Structure
```go
type Client struct {
    BaseURL     string          // Server base URL
    Token       string          // Authentication token
    ServerAlias string          // Configured server alias
    HTTPClient  *http.Client    // HTTP client with optional TLS config
}
```

#### Client Initialization
- `NewClient()` - Loads configuration and uses active server
- `NewClientWithToken(baseURL, token string, insecure bool)` - Direct initialization
- Supports `SANDCASTLE_HOST` environment variable for server override
- TLS verification can be disabled with `insecure` flag

#### HTTP Methods
- `do(method, path, body, result)` - Standard JSON request/response
- `doWithStatus(method, path, body)` - Returns status code and raw body
- Verbose logging via `VERBOSE` or `SANDCASTLE_VERBOSE` env vars
- Automatic error handling for HTTP 400+ responses
- Bearer token authentication in headers

### 5. Core Commands

#### Sandbox Management (cmd/sandbox.go)

**create [name]**
- Creates sandbox with auto-generated name if not provided (temp-{timestamp})
- Flags:
  - `--image` - Container image (default: ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest)
  - `--from-snapshot` - Restore from snapshot
  - `--restore-layers` - Comma-separated: container,home,data
  - `--tailscale` - Enable Tailscale
  - `--home` - Mount persistent home
  - `--data` - Mount user data directory
  - `--no-vnc` - Disable VNC
  - `--vnc-geometry` - VNC resolution
  - `--vnc-depth` - Color depth
  - `--no-docker` - Disable Docker daemon
  - `--smb` - Enable SMB sharing
  - `--rm` - Auto-remove on exit
- Environment variables: `SANDCASTLE_HOME`, `SANDCASTLE_DATA`, `SANDCASTLE_RM`
- Auto-connects to sandbox via SSH/mosh after creation
- Config preferences override defaults, command flags override everything

**list [--archived]**
- Lists all active sandboxes in tabular format
- Shows: NAME, STATUS, CREATED, ROUTE, TAILSCALE IP, IMAGE AGE
- `--archived` flag lists soft-deleted sandboxes

**delete <name>** [aliases: rm, d]
- Deletes a sandbox
- `-f, --force` - Skip confirmation prompt

**start <name>** [alias: up]
- Starts a stopped sandbox

**stop <name>** [alias: dn]
- Stops a running sandbox

**use [name]**
- Shows current server/sandbox if no arg
- Sets active sandbox by name
- Can also switch servers

**set <name> <temp|keep>**
- Toggles sandbox between temporary (auto-remove) and kept states

**rename <name> <new-name>** [alias: mv]
- Renames a sandbox

**unarchive <id>**
- Restores an archived sandbox from its volume

#### Route Management (cmd/route.go)

**route add <sandbox> [domain] [port]**
- Adds HTTP route (default) or TCP forward
- `--tcp` flag for TCP mode
- HTTP: `route add myapp app.example.com 8080` (default port 8080)
- TCP: `route add myapp --tcp 3000` (port required)
- Returns public port for TCP routes, domain + URL for HTTP

**route list <sandbox>**
- Lists all routes for a sandbox
- Shows: ID, MODE, DOMAIN/PUBLIC PORT, CONTAINER PORT, URL

**route delete <sandbox> [domain]**
- Deletes by domain or route ID
- `--id` flag to delete by route ID instead of domain

#### Connection Commands (cmd/connect.go)

**connect [name]** [alias: c]
- Connects to sandbox and attaches tmux
- Auto-starts if sandbox is stopped
- `--mosh` flag for mosh protocol (yes|no)
- Supports passthrough SSH options with `--`

**ssh [name]** [alias: s]
- SSH into sandbox without tmux
- `--mosh` flag for mosh protocol

Connection features:
- Auto-reachability check (3s timeout)
- SSH readiness wait (30s timeout with heartbeat)
- Protocol selection: SSH (default, with agent forwarding) or mosh
- Config-driven SSH extra args
- Disabled StrictHostKeyChecking for ease of use

#### File Operations

**exec <name> -- <command...>** [alias: x]
- Executes single command in sandbox
- Returns with command exit code

**cp <src> <dst>** [flags: -r, --recursive]
- Copies files via scp
- `sandbox:path` syntax for sandbox files
- Supports recursive directory copy
- Wrapper around scp with SSH options pre-configured

#### Service Management (cmd/services.go)

**docker start <sandbox>**
- Starts Docker daemon inside sandbox
- `--save` - Persist setting after restart

**docker stop <sandbox>**
- Stops Docker daemon

**vnc start <sandbox>**
- Starts VNC display server
- `--save` - Persist setting

**vnc stop <sandbox>**
- Stops VNC display server

#### Snapshot Management (cmd/snapshot.go)

**snapshot create <sandbox> [name]**
- Creates composite snapshot from container layers
- `--label` - Human-readable description
- `--layers` - Comma-separated: container,home,data
- `--data-subdir` - Snapshot only subdirectory

**snapshot list** [alias: ls]
- Lists all snapshots with size and creation date

**snapshot show <name>**
- Detailed snapshot information including layer breakdown

**snapshot delete/destroy <name>**
- Deletes a snapshot (aliases supported)

**snapshot restore <sandbox> <snapshot>**
- Restores sandbox from snapshot
- `--layers` - Selective layer restoration

#### Tailscale Integration (cmd/tailscale.go)

**tailscale enable** [alias: ts enable]
- Enables Tailscale sidecar
- `--auth-key` - Non-interactive with auth key
- Interactive mode: opens browser URL for auth

**tailscale disable** [alias: ts disable]
- Disables Tailscale sidecar

**tailscale status** [alias: ts status]
- Shows Tailscale connection state
- Lists connected sandboxes with bridge IPs

**tailscale connect <sandbox>**
- Connects sandbox to Tailscale network

**tailscale disconnect <sandbox>**
- Disconnects sandbox from Tailscale

#### API Token Management (cmd/token.go)

**token list**
- Lists all user API tokens
- Shows: ID, NAME, PREFIX, CREATED, LAST USED

**token create <name>**
- Creates new token with email/password auth
- Displays raw token (save immediately, can't retrieve later)

**token revoke <id>**
- Revokes an API token by ID

#### User Administration (cmd/users.go)

**users list** [alias: ls]
- Lists all users
- Shows: NAME, EMAIL, ADMIN, SANDBOXES, STATUS

**users create <name> <email> <password>**
- Creates new user with password

**users destroy <name>**
- Deletes user and their sandboxes

#### SMB File Sharing (cmd/smb.go)

**smb set-password**
- Sets SMB authentication password for file sharing
- Required before using `--smb` flag on sandbox creation
- Updates all SMB-enabled sandboxes

#### Server and Configuration Management (cmd/config.go)

**config show**
- Shows current config with source annotation (env/config/default)
- Displays active server, token (truncated), all preferences

**config set <key> <value>**
- Sets CLI preferences to ~/.sandcastle/config.yaml
- Valid keys: connect_protocol, use_tmux, ssh_extra_args, mount_home, data_path, vnc, docker
- Env vars override config at runtime

**server list** [alias: ls]
- Lists configured servers with auth status

**server use <alias>**
- Sets active server by alias or URL

**server remove <alias>** [alias: rm]
- Removes configured server

**login <url> [alias]**
- Authenticates with Sandcastle server via device flow
- Prompts for alias if not provided (derived from hostname)
- Opens browser automatically (macOS/Linux/Windows)
- Saves token to config
- `--insecure, -k` - Skips TLS verification

#### System Information

**info** [alias: i]
- Shows server info: Version, Rails, Ruby, Docker
- Host: Uptime, CPUs, Load, Memory, Disk
- Sandboxes: running/stopped/total, container counts
- Users: total and admin count

**status** [alias: st]
- Shows system status as JSON
- Docker info, sandbox counts, resource details

### 6. API Types (api/types.go)

#### Sandbox Management
- `Sandbox` - Complete sandbox state
- `CreateSandboxRequest` - Creation parameters
- `UpdateSandboxRequest` - Update toggles (name, temporary flag)

#### Routes
- `SandboxRoute` - Individual route metadata
- `RouteRequest` - Route creation params
- `RouteResponse` - Route response with URL

#### Connection
- `ConnectInfo` - SSH connection details (host, port, user, command, tailscale_ip)

#### Snapshots
- `Snapshot` - Snapshot metadata and layers
- `SnapshotRequest` - Snapshot creation params
- `CreateSnapshotRequest` - Explicit creation with sandbox ID
- `RestoreRequest` - Snapshot restore params

#### Authentication
- `DeviceCodeRequest/Response` - Device auth flow
- `DeviceTokenRequest/Response` - Token polling
- `CreateTokenRequest` - API token creation

#### Tailscale
- `TailscaleEnableRequest` - Auth key submission
- `TailscaleLoginResponse` - Login URL
- `TailscaleLoginStatus` - Auth status polling
- `TailscaleStatus` - Sidecar status, connected sandboxes
- `TailscaleSandbox` - Sandbox bridge IP info

#### Users (Admin)
- `User` - User metadata
- `CreateUserRequest` - User creation with optional SSH key
- `Token` - API token with last-used tracking
- `CreateTokenRequest` - Email/password token creation

#### Server Info
- `ServerInfo` - Complete server state
- `ServerHostInfo` - CPU, memory, disk, load, uptime
- `ServerDockerInfo` - Container/image counts
- `ServerUserCounts` - User statistics

### 7. Configuration System

Config location: `~/.sandcastle/config.yaml`

Configuration includes:
- Multiple servers with aliases
- Active server
- API tokens per server
- User preferences:
  - `connect_protocol` (ssh/mosh)
  - `use_tmux` (bool)
  - `ssh_extra_args` (string)
  - `mount_home` (bool)
  - `data_path` (string)
  - `vnc` (bool)
  - `docker` (bool)

Priority order (highest to lowest):
1. Command-line flags
2. Environment variables
3. Config file preferences
4. Built-in defaults

### 8. Argument Handling Patterns

**Cobra Command Patterns Used**:
- `cobra.MaximumNArgs(1)` - 0 or 1 args
- `cobra.ExactArgs(1)` - Exactly 1 arg
- `cobra.RangeArgs(1, 3)` - 1-3 args
- `cobra.ArbitraryArgs` - Any number of args
- `cobra.MinimumNArgs(2)` - At least 2 args

**Flag Patterns**:
- Bool flags for toggles
- String flags for values
- `NoOptDefVal` for flags that work with or without values
- Lookup by flag name to check if explicitly provided

**Special Handling**:
- `--` separator for passthrough args (connect/ssh commands)
- Sandbox name resolution: looks up by name from list
- Config file override precedence clearly managed
- Environment variable checks with helper functions

### 9. Error Handling

- API errors parsed as JSON and returned with HTTP status code
- Missing sandboxes return clear error messages
- Password input uses `golang.org/x/term` for secure input
- Confirmation prompts for destructive operations (delete)
- Server reachability checks before connection
- SSH readiness heartbeat with 30s timeout

This is a complete, production-grade CLI with comprehensive sandbox management, API authentication, file operations, and service management capabilities.

1	package cmd
2	
3	import (
4		"fmt"
5		"os"
6	
7		"github.com/sandcastle/cli/api"
8		"github.com/spf13/cobra"
9	)
10	
11	var (
12		Version = "dev"
13		Commit  = "none"
14		Date    = "unknown"
15	)
16	
17	var rootCmd = &cobra.Command{
18		Use:           "sandcastle",
19		Short:         "Sandcastle — shared Docker sandbox platform",
20		Long:          "CLI for managing Sandcastle development sandboxes.",
21		SilenceUsage:  true,
22		SilenceErrors: true,
23	}
24	
25	func Execute() {
26		if err := rootCmd.Execute(); err != nil {
27			fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, err)
28			os.Exit(1)
29		}
30	}
31	
32	func init() {
33		rootCmd.Version = Version
34		rootCmd.AddCommand(versionCmd)
35	}
36	
37	func printServer(client *api.Client) {
38		if client.ServerAlias != "" {
39			fmt.Printf("Server: %s (%s)\n", client.ServerAlias, client.BaseURL)
40		} else {
41			fmt.Printf("Server: %s\n", client.BaseURL)
42		}
43	}
44	
45	var versionCmd = &cobra.Command{
46		Use:   "version",
47		Short: "Print version",
48		Run: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) {
49			if Commit != "none" && Date != "unknown" {
50				fmt.Printf("sandcastle %s (%s) built %s\n", Version, Commit, Date)
51			} else {
52				fmt.Println("sandcastle", Version)
53			}
54		},
55	}
56	
```

67	var createCmd = &cobra.Command{
68		Use:     "create [name]",
69		Aliases: []string{"cr"},
70		Short:   "Create a new sandbox",
71		Long: `Create a new sandbox.
72	
73	If no name is provided, creates a temporary sandbox with an auto-generated name like "temp-<timestamp>".
74	
75	Environment variables can set defaults for commonly used flags:
76	  SANDCASTLE_HOME=1    equivalent to --home
77	  SANDCASTLE_DATA=.    equivalent to --data (value is the subpath, "." or "1" for root)
78	  SANDCASTLE_RM=1      equivalent to --rm
79	
80	Flags explicitly passed on the command line take precedence over environment variables.`,
81		Args: cobra.MaximumNArgs(1),
82		PreRun: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) {
83			// Priority: explicit flag > env var > config preference
84			cfg, _ := config.Load()
85			prefs := cfg.LoadPreferences()
86	
87			if !cmd.Flags().Changed("home") {
88				if prefs.MountHome != nil && *prefs.MountHome {
89					sandboxHome = true
90				}
91			}
92			if !cmd.Flags().Changed("rm") && envTruthy("SANDCASTLE_RM") {
93				sandboxRemove = true
94			}
95			if !cmd.Flags().Changed("data") {
96				if prefs.DataPath != "" {
97					sandboxData = prefs.DataPath
98				}
99			}
100			if !cmd.Flags().Changed("no-vnc") {
101				if prefs.VNC != nil && !*prefs.VNC {
102					sandboxNoVNC = true
103				}
104			}
105			if !cmd.Flags().Changed("no-docker") {
106				if prefs.Docker != nil && !*prefs.Docker {
107					sandboxNoDocker = true
108				}
109			}
110		},
111		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
112			client, err := api.NewClient()
113			if err != nil {
114				return err
115			}
116			printServer(client)
117	
118			// Auto-generate name if not provided
119			var name string
120			autoGenerated := false
121			if len(args) == 0 {
122				name = fmt.Sprintf("temp-%d", time.Now().Unix())
123				autoGenerated = true
124				// Auto-generated sandboxes are temporary by default
125				if !cmd.Flags().Changed("rm") {
126					sandboxRemove = true
127				}
128			} else {
129				name = args[0]
130			}
131	
132			// Resolve snapshot flags: --from-snapshot takes precedence over --snapshot
133			fromSnap := sandboxFromSnapshot
134			if fromSnap == "" {
135				fromSnap = sandboxSnapshot
136			}
137	
138			var restoreLayers []string
139			if sandboxRestoreLayers != "" {
140				for _, l := range strings.Split(sandboxRestoreLayers, ",") {
141					restoreLayers = append(restoreLayers, strings.TrimSpace(l))
142				}
143			}
144	
145			sandbox, err := client.CreateSandbox(api.CreateSandboxRequest{
146				Name:          name,
147				Image:         sandboxImage,
148				FromSnapshot:  fromSnap,
149				RestoreLayers: restoreLayers,
150				Tailscale:     sandboxTailscale,
151				MountHome:     sandboxHome,
152				DataPath:      sandboxData,
153				Temporary:     sandboxRemove,
154				VNCEnabled:    !sandboxNoVNC,
155				VNCGeometry:   sandboxVNCGeometry,
156				VNCDepth:      sandboxVNCDepth,
157				DockerEnabled: !sandboxNoDocker,
158				SMBEnabled:    sandboxSMB,
159			})
160			if err != nil {
161				return err
162			}
163	
164			if autoGenerated {
165				fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q created (auto-generated name).\n", sandbox.Name)
166			} else {
167				fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q created.\n", sandbox.Name)
168			}
169	
170			// Print active options (use local flags — they reflect what was actually requested)
171			if sandboxHome || sandboxData != "" || sandbox.Tailscale || sandboxRemove || fromSnap != "" || sandboxNoVNC || sandboxVNCGeometry != "" || sandboxVNCDepth != 0 || sandboxNoDocker || sandboxSMB {
172				if sandboxHome {
173					fmt.Println("  Home:      mounted (~/ persisted)")
174				}
175				if sandboxData != "" {
176					label := sandboxData
177					if label == "." {
178						label = "user data root"
179					}
180					fmt.Printf("  Data:      mounted (%s → /persisted)\n", label)
181				}
182				if sandbox.Tailscale {
183					fmt.Println("  Tailscale: enabled")
184				}
185				if sandboxRemove {
186					fmt.Println("  Cleanup:   auto-remove on exit")
187				}
188				if fromSnap != "" {
189					fmt.Printf("  Snapshot:  restored from %q\n", fromSnap)
190				}
191				if sandboxNoDocker {
192					fmt.Println("  Docker:    disabled")
193				}
194				if sandboxNoVNC {
195					fmt.Println("  VNC:       disabled")
196				} else if sandboxVNCGeometry != "" || sandboxVNCDepth != 0 {
197					geom := sandbox.VNCGeometry
198					if geom == "" {
199						geom = "1280x900"
200					}
201					depth := sandbox.VNCDepth
202					if depth == 0 {
203						depth = 24
204					}
205					fmt.Printf("  VNC:       %s @ %d-bit\n", geom, depth)
206				}
207				if sandboxSMB {
208					fmt.Println("  SMB:       enabled")
209				}
210			}
211	
212			if sandboxNoConnect {
213				return nil
214			}
215	
216			info, err := client.ConnectInfo(sandbox.ID)
217			if err != nil {
218				return err
219			}
220	
221			if os.Getenv("VERBOSE") == "1" {
222				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\033[2m[verbose] Connection info: host=%s port=%d user=%s\033[0m\n", info.Host, info.Port, info.User)
223				if info.TailscaleIP != "" {
224					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\033[2m[verbose] Tailscale IP (Tailscale): %s\033[0m\n", info.TailscaleIP)
225				}
226			}
227	
228			if err := checkHostReachable(info.Host, info.Port); err != nil {
229				return err
230			}
231	
232			if err := waitForSSH(info.Host, info.Port); err != nil {
233				return err
234			}
235	
236			cfg, loadErr := config.Load()
237			if loadErr != nil {
238				return loadErr
239			}
240			prefs := cfg.LoadPreferences()
241	
242			var remoteCmd string
243			if *prefs.UseTmux {
244				remoteCmd = tmuxCmd
245			}
246	
247			var sshErr error
248			if pickProtocol(cfg, info.Host, info.Port, info.User, prefs.SSHExtraArgs) == "mosh" {
249				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\033[33mWarning:\033[0m mosh does not support SSH agent forwarding. Use --mosh=no if you need ssh-add keys inside the sandbox.\n")
250				sshErr = moshExec(info.Host, info.Port, info.User, remoteCmd, prefs.SSHExtraArgs, nil)
251			} else {
252				sshErr = sshExec(info.Host, info.Port, info.User, remoteCmd, prefs.SSHExtraArgs, nil)
253			}
254	
255			if sandboxRemove {
256				// Re-fetch to check if user toggled to "keep" during the session
257				current, fetchErr := client.GetSandbox(sandbox.ID)
258				if fetchErr == nil && !current.Temporary {
259					fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q was set to keep — skipping removal.\n", sandbox.Name)
260				} else {
261					fmt.Printf("Removing sandbox %q...\n", sandbox.Name)
262					if err := client.DestroySandbox(sandbox.ID); err != nil {
263						fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to delete sandbox: %v\n", err)
264					} else {
265						fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q deleted.\n", sandbox.Name)
266					}
267				}
268			}
269	
270			return sshErr
271		},
272	}

274	var listCmd = &cobra.Command{
275		Use:     "list",
276		Aliases: []string{"ls"},
277		Short:   "List all sandboxes",
278		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
279			client, err := api.NewClient()
280			if err != nil {
281				return err
282			}
283			printServer(client)
284	
285			if listArchived {
286				sandboxes, err := client.ListArchivedSandboxes()
287				if err != nil {
288					return err
289				}
290				if len(sandboxes) == 0 {
291					fmt.Println("No archived sandboxes.")
292					return nil
293				}
294				w := tabwriter.NewWriter(os.Stdout, 0, 0, 2, ' ', 0)
295				fmt.Fprintln(w, "ID\tNAME\tARCHIVED\tCREATED\tIMAGE")
296				for _, s := range sandboxes {
297					archivedAt := ""
298					if s.ArchivedAt != nil {
299						archivedAt = s.ArchivedAt.Local().Format("2006-01-02 15:04")
300					}
301					created := s.CreatedAt.Local().Format("2006-01-02 15:04")
302					fmt.Fprintf(w, "%d\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n", s.ID, s.Name, archivedAt, created, s.Image)
303				}
304				w.Flush()
305				return nil
306			}
307	
308			sandboxes, err := client.ListSandboxes()
309			if err != nil {
310				return err
311			}
312	
313			if len(sandboxes) == 0 {
314				fmt.Println("No sandboxes.")
315				return nil
316			}
317	
318			hasRoute := false
319			for _, s := range sandboxes {
320				if len(s.Routes) > 0 {
321					hasRoute = true
322					break
323				}
324			}
325	
326			w := tabwriter.NewWriter(os.Stdout, 0, 0, 2, ' ', 0)
327			if hasRoute {
328				fmt.Fprintln(w, "NAME\tSTATUS\tCREATED\tROUTE\tTAILSCALE IP\tIMAGE AGE")
329			} else {
330				fmt.Fprintln(w, "NAME\tSTATUS\tCREATED\tTAILSCALE IP\tIMAGE AGE")
331			}
332			for _, s := range sandboxes {
333				name := s.Name
334				if s.Temporary {
335					name += " (temp)"
336				}
337				tsIP := ""
338				if s.TailscaleIP != "" {
339					tsIP = s.TailscaleIP
340				}
341				created  := s.CreatedAt.Local().Format("2006-01-02 15:04")
342				imageAge := formatImageAge(s.ImageBuiltAt)
343				if hasRoute {
344					route := ""
345					if len(s.Routes) > 0 {
346						parts := make([]string, len(s.Routes))
347						for i, r := range s.Routes {
348							parts[i] = fmt.Sprintf("%s (:%d)", r.URL, r.Port)
349						}
350						route = strings.Join(parts, ", ")
351					}
352					fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n", name, s.Status, created, route, tsIP, imageAge)
353				} else {
354					fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n", name, s.Status, created, tsIP, imageAge)
355				}
356			}
357			w.Flush()
358			return nil
359		},
360	}

483	var useCmd = &cobra.Command{
484		Use:   "use [name]",
485		Short: "Show or set active server/sandbox",
486		Long: `Without arguments, shows the current server and active sandbox.
487	With an argument, switches the active server or sandbox.
488	
489	Examples:
490	  sandcastle use                  # Show current server and sandbox
491	  sandcastle use my-sandbox       # Set active sandbox
492	  sandcastle use prod             # Switch to server "prod" (if configured)`,
493		Args: cobra.MaximumNArgs(1),
494		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
495			cfg, err := config.Load()
496			if err != nil {
497				return err
498			}
499	
500			// No args: list all servers, highlight active
501			if len(args) == 0 {
502				if len(cfg.Servers) == 0 {
503					fmt.Println("No servers configured — run: sandcastle login <url>")
504					return nil
505				}
506	
507				aliases := make([]string, 0, len(cfg.Servers))
508				for alias := range cfg.Servers {
509					aliases = append(aliases, alias)
510				}
511				sort.Strings(aliases)
512	
513				for _, alias := range aliases {
514					srv := cfg.Servers[alias]
515					if alias == cfg.CurrentServer {
516						fmt.Printf("  \033[1m* %-16s\033[0m %s\n", alias, srv.URL)
517					} else {
518						fmt.Printf("    %-16s %s\n", alias, srv.URL)
519					}
520				}
521	
522				return nil
523			}
524	
525			name := args[0]
526	
527			// Check if name matches a server alias or URL
528			if _, ok := cfg.Servers[name]; ok {
529				cfg.CurrentServer = name
530				if err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {
531					return err
532				}
533				fmt.Printf("Switched to server %s (%s)\n", name, cfg.Servers[name].URL)
534				return nil
535			}
536	
537			// Try matching by URL
538			for alias, srv := range cfg.Servers {
539				if srv.URL == strings.TrimRight(name, "/") {
540					cfg.CurrentServer = alias
541					if err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {
542						return err
543					}
544					fmt.Printf("Switched to server %s (%s)\n", alias, srv.URL)
545					return nil
546				}
547			}
548	
549			return fmt.Errorf("server %q not found — run: sandcastle use", name)
550		},
551	}
552	
553	var setCmd = &cobra.Command{

555		Short: "Toggle sandbox between temporary and kept",
556		Long: `Toggle a sandbox between temporary (auto-remove on exit) and kept.
557	
558	  temp   Mark as temporary — will be removed when the CLI session exits
559	  keep   Mark as kept — will not be auto-removed`,
560		Args: cobra.ExactArgs(2),

562			client, err := api.NewClient()

564				return err
565			}
566			printServer(client)
567	
568			sandbox, err := findSandboxByName(client, args[0])
569			if err != nil {
570				return err
571			}
572	
573			mode := strings.ToLower(args[1])
574			var temp bool
575			switch mode {
576			case "temp":
577				temp = true
578			case "keep":
579				temp = false
580			default:
581				return fmt.Errorf("unknown mode %q: use \"temp\" or \"keep\"", args[1])
582			}
583	
584			sandbox, err = client.UpdateSandbox(sandbox.ID, api.UpdateSandboxRequest{Temporary: &temp})
585			if err != nil {
586				return err
587			}
588	
589			if temp {

591			} else {
592				fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q set to keep (will not be removed on exit).\n", sandbox.Name)
593			}
594			return nil
595		},
596	}

625	
626	// formatImageAge returns a human-readable image age string (e.g. "2h ago", "3d ago").
627	// Returns "-" when the build timestamp is not available.
628	func formatImageAge(builtAt *time.Time) string {
629		if builtAt == nil {
630			return "-"
631		}
632		d := time.Since(*builtAt)
633		switch {
634		case d < time.Minute:
635			return "just now"
636		case d < time.Hour:
637			return fmt.Sprintf("%dm ago", int(d.Minutes()))
638		case d < 24*time.Hour:
639			return fmt.Sprintf("%dh ago", int(d.Hours()))
640		default:
641			return fmt.Sprintf("%dd ago", int(d.Hours()/24))
642		}
643	}
644	
645	func envTruthy(key string) bool {
646		v := strings.ToLower(os.Getenv(key))
647		return v == "1" || v == "true" || v == "yes"
648	}
649	
650	func findSandboxByName(client *api.Client, name string) (*api.Sandbox, error) {
651		sandboxes, err := client.ListSandboxes()
652		if err != nil {
653			return nil, err
654		}
655		for _, s := range sandboxes {
656			if s.Name == name {
657				return &s, nil
658			}
659		}
660		return nil, fmt.Errorf("sandbox %q not found", name)
661	}

24	var routeCmd = &cobra.Command{
25		Use:   "route",
26		Short: "Manage custom domain routes for a sandbox",
27		Long: `Add, list, or remove custom domain routes for a sandbox.
28	Each sandbox can have multiple routes pointing to different ports.
29	
30	Examples:
31	  sandcastle route add myapp app.example.com         # Add HTTP route (default port 8080)
32	  sandcastle route add myapp api.example.com 3000    # Add HTTP route on custom port
33	  sandcastle route add myapp --tcp 3000              # Add TCP forward to container port 3000
34	  sandcastle route list myapp                        # List all routes
35	  sandcastle route delete myapp app.example.com      # Remove an HTTP route by domain
36	  sandcastle route delete myapp --id 42              # Remove any route by ID`,
37	}

39	var routeAddCmd = &cobra.Command{
40		Use:   "add <sandbox> [domain] [port]",
41		Short: "Add a custom domain or TCP route",
42		Args:  cobra.RangeArgs(1, 3),
43		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
44			client, err := api.NewClient()
45			if err != nil {
46				return err
47			}
48			printServer(client)
49

51			if err != nil {
52				return err
53			}
54	
55			req := api.RouteRequest{}
56	
57			if routeAddTCP {
58				// TCP mode: sandcastle route add <sandbox> --tcp <port>
59				req.Mode = "tcp"
60				port := 8080
61				if len(args) >= 2 {
62					port, err = strconv.Atoi(args[1])
63					if err != nil {
64						return fmt.Errorf("invalid port: %s", args[1])
65					}
66				}
67				req.Port = port
68			} else {
69				// HTTP mode: sandcastle route add <sandbox> <domain> [port]
70				if len(args) < 2 {
71					return fmt.Errorf("HTTP routes require a domain argument")
72				}
73				req.Mode = "http"
74				req.Domain = args[1]
75				req.Port = 8080
76				if len(args) == 3 {
77					req.Port, err = strconv.Atoi(args[2])
78					if err != nil {
79						return fmt.Errorf("invalid port: %s", args[2])
80					}
81				}
82			}
83	
84			route, err := client.AddRoute(sandbox.ID, req)
85			if err != nil {
86				return err
87			}
88	
89			fmt.Printf("Route added for sandbox %q.\n", sandbox.Name)
90			if route.Mode == "tcp" {
91				fmt.Printf("  Mode:           TCP\n")
92				fmt.Printf("  Public port:    %d\n", route.PublicPort)
93				fmt.Printf("  Container port: %d\n", route.Port)
94				fmt.Println("\nConnect via TCP: <host>:" + strconv.Itoa(route.PublicPort))
95			} else {

97				fmt.Printf("  Port:   %d\n", route.Port)
98				fmt.Printf("  URL:    %s\n", route.URL)
99				fmt.Println("\nPoint your DNS to this server, and Traefik will handle TLS automatically.")
100			}
101			return nil
102		},
103	}

1	package api
2	
3	import (
4		"bytes"
5		"crypto/tls"
6		"encoding/json"
7		"fmt"
8		"io"
9		"net/http"
10		"os"
11		"strings"
12	
13		"github.com/sandcastle/cli/internal/config"
14	)
15	
16	func verbose() bool {
17		// Check both VERBOSE and SANDCASTLE_VERBOSE
18		v := strings.ToLower(os.Getenv("VERBOSE"))
19		if v == "" {
20			v = strings.ToLower(os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_VERBOSE"))
21		}
22		return v == "1" || v == "true"
23	}
24	
25	func logVerbose(format string, args ...any) {
26		if verbose() {
27			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\033[2m"+format+"\033[0m\n", args...)
28		}
29	}
30	
31	type Client struct {
32		BaseURL     string
33		Token       string
34		ServerAlias string
35		HTTPClient  *http.Client
36	}
37	
38	func newHTTPClient(insecure bool) *http.Client {
39		if insecure {
40			return &http.Client{
41				Transport: &http.Transport{
42					TLSClientConfig: &tls.Config{InsecureSkipVerify: true},
43				},
44			}
45		}
46		return &http.Client{}
47	}
48	
49	func NewClient() (*Client, error) {
50		cfg, err := config.Load()
51		if err != nil {
52			return nil, err
53		}
54	
55		// SANDCASTLE_HOST overrides the active server — accepts a server alias or URL.
56		if host := os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_HOST"); host != "" {
57			// 1. Alias match
58			if srv, ok := cfg.Servers[host]; ok {
59				logVerbose("server (SANDCASTLE_HOST alias): %s (%s)", host, srv.URL)
60				return &Client{
61					BaseURL:     srv.URL,
62					Token:       srv.Token,
63					ServerAlias: host,
64					HTTPClient:  newHTTPClient(srv.Insecure),
65				}, nil
66			}
67			// 2. URL match — reuse stored token for that server
68			normalized := strings.TrimRight(host, "/")
69			for alias, srv := range cfg.Servers {
70				if strings.TrimRight(srv.URL, "/") == normalized {
71					logVerbose("server (SANDCASTLE_HOST url): %s (%s)", alias, normalized)
72					return &Client{
73						BaseURL:     normalized,
74						Token:       srv.Token,
75						ServerAlias: alias,
76						HTTPClient:  newHTTPClient(srv.Insecure),
77					}, nil
78				}
79			}
80			// 3. Unknown URL — use without auth
81			logVerbose("server (SANDCASTLE_HOST, unauthenticated): %s", normalized)
82			return &Client{
83				BaseURL:    normalized,
84				HTTPClient: newHTTPClient(false),
85			}, nil
86		}
87	
88		srv, err := cfg.CurrentServerConfig()
89		if err != nil {
90			return nil, err
91		}
92		logVerbose("server: %s (%s)", cfg.CurrentServer, srv.URL)
93		return &Client{
94			BaseURL:     srv.URL,
95			Token:       srv.Token,
96			ServerAlias: cfg.CurrentServer,
97			HTTPClient:  newHTTPClient(srv.Insecure),
98		}, nil
99	}
100	
101	func NewClientWithToken(baseURL, token string, insecure bool) *Client {
102		return &Client{
103			BaseURL:    baseURL,
104			Token:      token,
105			HTTPClient: newHTTPClient(insecure),
106		}
107	}
108	
109	func (c *Client) do(method, path string, body any, result any) error {
110		var bodyReader io.Reader
111		var reqData []byte
112		if body != nil {
113			var err error
114			reqData, err = json.Marshal(body)
115			if err != nil {
116				return fmt.Errorf("marshaling request: %w", err)
117			}
118			bodyReader = bytes.NewReader(reqData)
119		}
120	
121		logVerbose("→ %s %s%s", method, c.BaseURL, path)
122		if len(reqData) > 0 {
123			logVerbose("  request: %s", string(reqData))
124		}
125	
126		req, err := http.NewRequest(method, c.BaseURL+path, bodyReader)
127		if err != nil {
128			return fmt.Errorf("creating request: %w", err)
129		}
130	
131		req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
132		if c.Token != "" {
133			req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+c.Token)
134		}
135	
136		resp, err := c.HTTPClient.Do(req)
137		if err != nil {
138			return fmt.Errorf("request failed: %w", err)
139		}
140		defer resp.Body.Close()
141	
142		respBody, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
143		if err != nil {
144			return fmt.Errorf("reading response: %w", err)
145		}
146	
147		logVerbose("← %d (%d bytes)", resp.StatusCode, len(respBody))
148		if len(respBody) > 0 {
149			logVerbose("  response: %s", string(respBody))
150		}
151	
152		if resp.StatusCode >= 400 {
153			var apiErr APIError
154			if json.Unmarshal(respBody, &apiErr) == nil && apiErr.Error != "" {
155				return fmt.Errorf("API error (%d): %s", resp.StatusCode, apiErr.Error)
156			}
157			return fmt.Errorf("API error (%d): %s", resp.StatusCode, string(respBody))
158		}
159	
160		if result != nil {
161			if err := json.Unmarshal(respBody, result); err != nil {
162				return fmt.Errorf("parsing response: %w", err)
163			}
164		}
165		return nil
166	}
167	
168	// doWithStatus is like do but returns the HTTP status code along with the response body.
169	func (c *Client) doWithStatus(method, path string, body any) (int, []byte, error) {
170		var bodyReader io.Reader
171		var reqData []byte
172		if body != nil {
173			var err error
174			reqData, err = json.Marshal(body)
175			if err != nil {
176				return 0, nil, fmt.Errorf("marshaling request: %w", err)
177			}
178			bodyReader = bytes.NewReader(reqData)
179		}
180	
181		logVerbose("→ %s %s%s", method, c.BaseURL, path)
182		if len(reqData) > 0 {
183			logVerbose("  request: %s", string(reqData))
184		}
185	
186		req, err := http.NewRequest(method, c.BaseURL+path, bodyReader)
187		if err != nil {
188			return 0, nil, fmt.Errorf("creating request: %w", err)
189		}
190	
191		req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
192		if c.Token != "" {
193			req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+c.Token)
194		}
195	
196		resp, err := c.HTTPClient.Do(req)
197		if err != nil {
198			return 0, nil, fmt.Errorf("request failed: %w", err)
199		}
200		defer resp.Body.Close()
201	
202		respBody, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
203		if err != nil {
204			return 0, nil, fmt.Errorf("reading response: %w", err)
205		}
206	
207		logVerbose("← %d (%d bytes)", resp.StatusCode, len(respBody))
208		if len(respBody) > 0 {
209			logVerbose("  response: %s", string(respBody))
210		}
211	
212		return resp.StatusCode, respBody, nil
213	}
214	
215	// Device Auth
216	
217	func (c *Client) RequestDeviceCode(clientName string) (*DeviceCodeResponse, error) {
218		var resp DeviceCodeResponse
219		err := c.do("POST", "/api/auth/device_code", DeviceCodeRequest{ClientName: clientName}, &resp)
220		return &resp, err
221	}
222	
223	func (c *Client) PollDeviceToken(deviceCode string) (token string, pending bool, err error) {
224		status, body, err := c.doWithStatus("POST", "/api/auth/device_token", DeviceTokenRequest{DeviceCode: deviceCode})
225		if err != nil {
226			return "", false, err
227		}
228	
229		switch status {
230		case 200:
231			var resp DeviceTokenResponse
232			if err := json.Unmarshal(body, &resp); err != nil {
233				return "", false, fmt.Errorf("parsing response: %w", err)
234			}
235			return resp.Token, false, nil
236		case 428: // Precondition Required — authorization_pending
237			return "", true, nil
238		default:
239			var apiErr APIError
240			if json.Unmarshal(body, &apiErr) == nil && apiErr.Error != "" {
241				return "", false, fmt.Errorf("%s", apiErr.Error)
242			}
243			return "", false, fmt.Errorf("unexpected status %d: %s", status, string(body))
244		}
245	}

311	func (c *Client) ServiceStop(sandboxID int, service string, save bool) (*Sandbox, error) {
312		path := fmt.Sprintf("/api/sandboxes/%d/services/%s/stop", sandboxID, service)
313		if save {
314			path += "?save=1"
315		}

317		err := c.do("POST", path, nil, &s)
318		return &s, err
319	}

343	}
344	
345	func (c *Client) RemoveRoute(sandboxID int, domain string) error {
346		routes, err := c.ListRoutes(sandboxID)
347		if err != nil {
348			return err
349		}
350		for _, r := range routes {
351			if r.Domain == domain {
352				return c.RemoveRouteByID(sandboxID, r.ID)
353			}
354		}
355		return fmt.Errorf("route with domain %q not found", domain)
356	}

lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"42\"))\n\n\theaderStyle = lipgloss.NewStyle().\n\t\t\tBold(true).\n\t\t\tForeground(lipgloss.Color(\"75\"))\n\n\trouteDomainStyle = lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"117\"))\n)\n\n// ---------- views ----------\n\ntype tuiView int\n\nconst (\n\tviewSandboxes tuiView = iota\n\tviewRoutes\n\tviewCreateSandbox\n\tviewAddRoute\n\tviewConfirmDelete\n)\n\n// ---------- messages ----------\n\ntype sandboxesLoadedMsg struct {\n\tsandboxes []api.Sandbox\n\terr       error\n}\n\ntype routesLoadedMsg struct {\n\troutes []api.RouteResponse\n\terr    error\n}\n\ntype actionDoneMsg struct {\n\tmsg string\n\terr error\n}\n\n// ---------- model ----------\n\ntype tuiModel struct {\n\tclient *api.Client\n\n\tview     tuiView\n\tcursor   int\n\twidth    int\n\theight   int\n\tspinner  spinner.Model\n\tloading  bool\n\tfeedback string\n\tfeedErr  bool\n\n\t// sandbox list\n\tsandboxes []api.Sandbox\n\n\t// routes\n\trouteSandbox *api.Sandbox\n\troutes       []api.RouteResponse\n\trouteCursor  int\n\n\t// create sandbox\n\tcreateInput textinput.Model\n\n\t// add route\n\trouteInputs    [2]textinput.Model // domain, port\n\trouteFocusIdx  int\n\n\t// confirm delete\n\tdeleteTarget string\n\tdeleteID     int\n}\n\nfunc newTUI(client *api.Client) tuiModel {\n\ts := spinner.New()\n\ts.Spinner = spinner.Dot\n\ts.Style = lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"214\"))\n\n\tti := textinput.New()\n\tti.Placeholder = \"sandbox name (leave empty for auto)\"\n\tti.CharLimit = 40\n\tti.Width = 40\n\n\tdomainInput := textinput.New()\n\tdomainInput.Placeholder = \"domain (e.g. app.example.com)\"\n\tdomainInput.CharLimit = 80\n\tdomainInput.Width = 40\n\n\tportInput := textinput.New()\n\tportInput.Placeholder = \"port (default 8080)\"\n\tportInput.CharLimit = 5\n\tportInput.Width = 10\n\n\treturn tuiModel{\n\t\tclient:      client,\n\t\tspinner:     s,\n\t\tloading:     true,\n\t\tcreateInput: ti,\n\t\trouteInputs: [2]textinput.Model{domainInput, portInput},\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) Init() tea.Cmd {\n\treturn tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, loadSandboxes(m.client))\n}\n\n// ---------- commands ----------\n\nfunc loadSandboxes(client *api.Client) tea.Cmd {\n\treturn func() tea.Msg {\n\t\tsandboxes, err := client.ListSandboxes()\n\t\treturn sandboxesLoadedMsg{sandboxes, err}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc loadRoutes(client *api.Client, sandboxID int) tea.Cmd {\n\treturn func() tea.Msg {\n\t\troutes, err := client.ListRoutes(sandboxID)\n\t\treturn routesLoadedMsg{routes, err}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc doAction(fn func() (string, error)) tea.Cmd {\n\treturn func() tea.Msg {\n\t\tmsg, err := fn()\n\t\treturn actionDoneMsg{msg, err}\n\t}\n}\n\n// ---------- update ----------\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n\tcase tea.WindowSizeMsg:\n\t\tm.width = msg.Width\n\t\tm.height = msg.Height\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\n\tcase spinner.TickMsg:\n\t\tvar cmd tea.Cmd\n\t\tm.spinner, cmd = m.spinner.Update(msg)\n\t\treturn m, cmd\n\n\tcase sandboxesLoadedMsg:\n\t\tm.loading = false\n\t\tif msg.err != nil {\n\t\t\tm.feedback = msg.err.Error()\n\t\t\tm.feedErr = true\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.sandboxes = msg.sandboxes\n\t\tif m.cursor >= len(m.sandboxes) {\n\t\t\tm.cursor = max(0, len(m.sandboxes)-1)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\n\tcase routesLoadedMsg:\n\t\tm.loading = false\n\t\tif msg.err != nil {\n\t\t\tm.feedback = msg.err.Error()\n\t\t\tm.feedErr = true\n\t\t\tm.view = viewSandboxes\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.routes = msg.routes\n\t\tm.routeCursor = 0\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\n\tcase actionDoneMsg:\n\t\tm.loading = false\n\t\tif msg.err != nil {\n\t\t\tm.feedback = msg.err.Error()\n\t\t\tm.feedErr = true\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tm.feedback = msg.msg\n\t\t\tm.feedErr = false\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Reload sandboxes\n\t\tif m.view == viewConfirmDelete {\n\t\t\tm.view = viewSandboxes\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.loading = true\n\t\treturn m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, loadSandboxes(m.client))\n\t}\n\n\tswitch m.view {\n\tcase viewSandboxes:\n\t\treturn m.updateSandboxes(msg)\n\tcase viewRoutes:\n\t\treturn m.updateRoutes(msg)\n\tcase viewCreateSandbox:\n\t\treturn m.updateCreate(msg)\n\tcase viewAddRoute:\n\t\treturn m.updateAddRoute(msg)\n\tcase viewConfirmDelete:\n\t\treturn m.updateConfirmDelete(msg)\n\t}\n\treturn m, nil\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) updateSandboxes(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n\tcase tea.KeyMsg:\n\t\tm.feedback = \"\"\n\t\tswitch {\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"q\", \"ctrl+c\"))):\n\t\t\treturn m, tea.Quit\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"up\", \"k\"))):\n\t\t\tif m.cursor > 0 {\n\t\t\t\tm.cursor--\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"down\", \"j\"))):\n\t\t\tif m.cursor < len(m.sandboxes)-1 {\n\t\t\t\tm.cursor++\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"c\"))):\n\t\t\tm.view = viewCreateSandbox\n\t\t\tm.createInput.SetValue(\"\")\n\t\t\tm.createInput.Focus()\n\t\t\treturn m, m.createInput.Cursor.BlinkCmd()\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"r\"))):\n\t\t\tif len(m.sandboxes) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\tm.loading = true\n\t\t\t\tm.feedback = \"\"\n\t\t\t\tsb := m.sandboxes[m.cursor]\n\t\t\t\tm.routeSandbox = &sb\n\t\t\t\tm.view = viewRoutes\n\t\t\t\treturn m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, loadRoutes(m.client, sb.ID))\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"R\"))):\n\t\t\tm.loading = true\n\t\t\tm.feedback = \"\"\n\t\t\treturn m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, loadSandboxes(m.client))\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"s\"))):\n\t\t\tif len(m.sandboxes) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\tsb := m.sandboxes[m.cursor]\n\t\t\t\tif sb.Status == \"stopped\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tm.loading = true\n\t\t\t\t\treturn m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\t_, err := m.client.StartSandbox(sb.ID)\n\t\t\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%q started\", sb.Name), err\n\t\t\t\t\t}))\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"x\"))):\n\t\t\tif len(m.sandboxes) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\tsb := m.sandboxes[m.cursor]\n\t\t\t\tif sb.Status == \"running\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tm.loading = true\n\t\t\t\t\treturn m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {\n\t\t\t\t\t\t_, err := m.client.StopSandbox(sb.ID)\n\t\t\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%q stopped\", sb.Name), err\n\t\t\t\t\t}))\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"d\"))):\n\t\t\tif len(m.sandboxes) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\tsb := m.sandboxes[m.cursor]\n\t\t\t\tm.deleteTarget = sb.Name\n\t\t\t\tm.deleteID = sb.ID\n\t\t\t\tm.view = viewConfirmDelete\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"enter\"))):\n\t\t\t// connect — exit TUI and SSH in\n\t\t\tif len(m.sandboxes) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\tsb := m.sandboxes[m.cursor]\n\t\t\t\t// We'll print the connect command and exit\n\t\t\t\treturn m, tea.Sequence(\n\t\t\t\t\ttea.Printf(\"Connecting to %s...\", sb.Name),\n\t\t\t\t\ttea.ExecProcess(connectExecCmd(sb.Name), func(err error) tea.Msg {\n\t\t\t\t\t\treturn actionDoneMsg{\"\", err}\n\t\t\t\t\t}),\n\t\t\t\t)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn m, nil\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) updateRoutes(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n\tcase tea.KeyMsg:\n\t\tm.feedback = \"\"\n\t\tswitch {\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"q\", \"esc\"))):\n\t\t\tm.view = viewSandboxes\n\t\t\tm.routes = nil\n\t\t\tm.routeSandbox = nil\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"ctrl+c\"))):\n\t\t\treturn m, tea.Quit\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"up\", \"k\"))):\n\t\t\tif m.routeCursor > 0 {\n\t\t\t\tm.routeCursor--\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"down\", \"j\"))):\n\t\t\tif m.routeCursor < len(m.routes)-1 {\n\t\t\t\tm.routeCursor++\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"a\"))):\n\t\t\tm.view = viewAddRoute\n\t\t\tm.routeFocusIdx = 0\n\t\t\tm.routeInputs[0].SetValue(\"\")\n\t\t\tm.routeInputs[1].SetValue(\"\")\n\t\t\tm.routeInputs[0].Focus()\n\t\t\tm.routeInputs[1].Blur()\n\t\t\treturn m, m.routeInputs[0].Cursor.BlinkCmd()\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"d\"))):\n\t\t\tif len(m.routes) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\tr := m.routes[m.routeCursor]\n\t\t\t\tsbID := m.routeSandbox.ID\n\t\t\t\tm.loading = true\n\t\t\t\treturn m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {\n\t\t\t\t\terr := m.client.RemoveRouteByID(sbID, r.ID)\n\t\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"Route #%d removed\", r.ID), err\n\t\t\t\t}))\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"R\"))):\n\t\t\tif m.routeSandbox != nil {\n\t\t\t\tm.loading = true\n\t\t\t\treturn m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, loadRoutes(m.client, m.routeSandbox.ID))\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn m, nil\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) updateCreate(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n\tcase tea.KeyMsg:\n\t\tswitch msg.Type {\n\t\tcase tea.KeyEsc:\n\t\t\tm.view = viewSandboxes\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\tcase tea.KeyCtrlC:\n\t\t\treturn m, tea.Quit\n\t\tcase tea.KeyEnter:\n\t\t\tname := strings.TrimSpace(m.createInput.Value())\n\t\t\tif name == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tname = fmt.Sprintf(\"temp-%d\", time.Now().Unix())\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tm.view = viewSandboxes\n\t\t\tm.loading = true\n\t\t\treturn m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {\n\t\t\t\tsb, err := m.client.CreateSandbox(api.CreateSandboxRequest{\n\t\t\t\t\tName:          name,\n\t\t\t\t\tImage:         \"ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest\",\n\t\t\t\t\tVNCEnabled:    true,\n\t\t\t\t\tDockerEnabled: true,\n\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%q created\", sb.Name), nil\n\t\t\t}))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tvar cmd tea.Cmd\n\tm.createInput, cmd = m.createInput.Update(msg)\n\treturn m, cmd\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) updateAddRoute(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n\tcase tea.KeyMsg:\n\t\tswitch msg.Type {\n\t\tcase tea.KeyEsc:\n\t\t\tm.view = viewRoutes\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\tcase tea.KeyCtrlC:\n\t\t\treturn m, tea.Quit\n\t\tcase tea.KeyTab, tea.KeyShiftTab:\n\t\t\tm.routeFocusIdx = (m.routeFocusIdx + 1) % 2\n\t\t\tfor i := range m.routeInputs {\n\t\t\t\tif i == m.routeFocusIdx {\n\t\t\t\t\tm.routeInputs[i].Focus()\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\tm.routeInputs[i].Blur()\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn m, m.routeInputs[m.routeFocusIdx].Cursor.BlinkCmd()\n\t\tcase tea.KeyEnter:\n\t\t\tdomain := strings.TrimSpace(m.routeInputs[0].Value())\n\t\t\tportStr := strings.TrimSpace(m.routeInputs[1].Value())\n\t\t\tif domain == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tm.feedback = \"domain is required\"\n\t\t\t\tm.feedErr = true\n\t\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tport := 8080\n\t\t\tif portStr != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tn := 0\n\t\t\t\tfor _, ch := range portStr {\n\t\t\t\t\tif ch < '0' || ch > '9' {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tm.feedback = \"invalid port\"\n\t\t\t\t\t\tm.feedErr = true\n\t\t\t\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\tn = n*10 + int(ch-'0')\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tport = n\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tsbID := m.routeSandbox.ID\n\t\t\tm.view = viewRoutes\n\t\t\tm.loading = true\n\t\t\treturn m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {\n\t\t\t\tr, err := m.client.AddRoute(sbID, api.RouteRequest{\n\t\t\t\t\tDomain: domain,\n\t\t\t\t\tPort:   port,\n\t\t\t\t\tMode:   \"http\",\n\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn \"\", err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"Route added: %s → :%d\", r.Domain, r.Port), nil\n\t\t\t}))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tvar cmd tea.Cmd\n\tm.routeInputs[m.routeFocusIdx], cmd = m.routeInputs[m.routeFocusIdx].Update(msg)\n\treturn m, cmd\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) updateConfirmDelete(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n\tcase tea.KeyMsg:\n\t\tswitch {\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"y\", \"Y\"))):\n\t\t\tid := m.deleteID\n\t\t\tname := m.deleteTarget\n\t\t\tm.loading = true\n\t\t\treturn m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {\n\t\t\t\terr := m.client.DestroySandbox(id)\n\t\t\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%q destroyed\", name), err\n\t\t\t}))\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\tm.view = viewSandboxes\n\t\t\tm.feedback = \"cancelled\"\n\t\t\tm.feedErr = false\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn m, nil\n}\n\n// ---------- view ----------\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) View() string {\n\tvar b strings.Builder\n\n\ttitle := titleStyle.Render(\"  Sandcastle\")\n\tif m.client.ServerAlias != \"\" {\n\t\ttitle += helpStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\"  %s\", m.client.ServerAlias))\n\t}\n\tb.WriteString(title + \"\\n\\n\")\n\n\tswitch m.view {\n\tcase viewSandboxes:\n\t\tm.viewSandboxes(&b)\n\tcase viewRoutes:\n\t\tm.viewRoutes(&b)\n\tcase viewCreateSandbox:\n\t\tm.viewCreate(&b)\n\tcase viewAddRoute:\n\t\tm.viewAddRoute(&b)\n\tcase viewConfirmDelete:\n\t\tm.viewConfirmDelete(&b)\n\t}\n\n\t// Feedback\n\tif m.feedback != \"\" {\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n\t\tif m.feedErr {\n\t\t\tb.WriteString(errStyle.Render(\"  \" + m.feedback))\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tb.WriteString(okStyle.Render(\"  \" + m.feedback))\n\t\t}\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n\t}\n\n\treturn b.String()\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) viewSandboxes(b *strings.Builder) {\n\tif m.loading && len(m.sandboxes) == 0 {\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"  \" + m.spinner.View() + \" Loading sandboxes...\\n\")\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\" + helpStyle.Render(\"  q quit\"))\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tif len(m.sandboxes) == 0 {\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"  No sandboxes. Press c to create one.\\n\")\n\t} else {\n\t\t// Header\n\t\tb.WriteString(headerStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\"  %-22s %-10s %-18s %s\", \"NAME\", \"STATUS\", \"CREATED\", \"ROUTE\")) + \"\\n\")\n\n\t\tfor i, sb := range m.sandboxes {\n\t\t\tname := sb.Name\n\t\t\tif sb.Temporary {\n\t\t\t\tname += \" ~\"\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif len(name) > 22 {\n\t\t\t\tname = name[:21] + \"…\"\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tst := statusStopped.Render(sb.Status)\n\t\t\tif sb.Status == \"running\" {\n\t\t\t\tst = statusRunning.Render(sb.Status)\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tcreated := sb.CreatedAt.Local().Format(\"Jan 02 15:04\")\n\n\t\t\troute := \"\"\n\t\t\tif len(sb.Routes) > 0 {\n\t\t\t\tparts := make([]string, 0, len(sb.Routes))\n\t\t\t\tfor _, r := range sb.Routes {\n\t\t\t\t\tif r.Mode == \"tcp\" {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tparts = append(parts, fmt.Sprintf(\"tcp/:%d\", r.PublicPort))\n\t\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tparts = append(parts, r.Domain)\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\troute = strings.Join(parts, \", \")\n\t\t\t\tif len(route) > 30 {\n\t\t\t\t\troute = route[:29] + \"…\"\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tline := fmt.Sprintf(\"  %-22s %-10s %-18s %s\", name, st, created, route)\n\t\t\tif i == m.cursor {\n\t\t\t\t// Re-render with selection styling — pad to width\n\t\t\t\tpadded := fmt.Sprintf(\"  %-22s %-20s %-18s %-30s\", name, sb.Status, created, route)\n\t\t\t\tline = selectedStyle.Render(padded)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tb.WriteString(line + \"\\n\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif m.loading {\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"\\n  \" + m.spinner.View() + \"\\n\")\n\t}\n\n\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n\tb.WriteString(helpStyle.Render(\"  enter connect  c create  s start  x stop  d destroy  r routes  R refresh  q quit\"))\n\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) viewRoutes(b *strings.Builder) {\n\tb.WriteString(headerStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\"  Routes for %s\", m.routeSandbox.Name)) + \"\\n\\n\")\n\n\tif m.loading {\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"  \" + m.spinner.View() + \" Loading routes...\\n\")\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\" + helpStyle.Render(\"  esc back  q quit\"))\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tif len(m.routes) == 0 {\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"  No routes. Press a to add one.\\n\")\n\t} else {\n\t\tb.WriteString(headerStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\"  %-6s %-6s %-30s %-8s %s\", \"ID\", \"MODE\", \"DOMAIN / PUBLIC PORT\", \"PORT\", \"URL\")) + \"\\n\")\n\t\tfor i, r := range m.routes {\n\t\t\ttarget := r.Domain\n\t\t\tif r.Mode == \"tcp\" {\n\t\t\t\ttarget = fmt.Sprintf(\":%d\", r.PublicPort)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\turl := r.URL\n\t\t\tif len(url) > 40 {\n\t\t\t\turl = url[:39] + \"…\"\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tline := fmt.Sprintf(\"  %-6d %-6s %-30s %-8d %s\", r.ID, r.Mode, target, r.Port, url)\n\t\t\tif i == m.routeCursor {\n\t\t\t\tline = selectedStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\"  %-6d %-6s %-30s %-8d %-40s\", r.ID, r.Mode, target, r.Port, url))\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tb.WriteString(line + \"\\n\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n\tb.WriteString(helpStyle.Render(\"  a add  d delete  R refresh  esc/q back\"))\n\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) viewCreate(b *strings.Builder) {\n\tb.WriteString(headerStyle.Render(\"  Create Sandbox\") + \"\\n\\n\")\n\tb.WriteString(\"  Name: \" + m.createInput.View() + \"\\n\")\n\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n\tb.WriteString(helpStyle.Render(\"  enter confirm  esc cancel\"))\n\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) viewAddRoute(b *strings.Builder) {\n\tb.WriteString(headerStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\"  Add Route to %s\", m.routeSandbox.Name)) + \"\\n\\n\")\n\tb.WriteString(\"  Domain: \" + m.routeInputs[0].View() + \"\\n\")\n\tb.WriteString(\"  Port:   \" + m.routeInputs[1].View() + \"\\n\")\n\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n\tb.WriteString(helpStyle.Render(\"  tab next field  enter confirm  esc cancel\"))\n\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) viewConfirmDelete(b *strings.Builder) {\n\tb.WriteString(errStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\"  Destroy sandbox %q?\", m.deleteTarget)) + \"\\n\\n\")\n\tb.WriteString(\"  Press y to confirm, any other key to cancel.\\n\")\n}\n\n// ---------- connect helper ----------\n\nfunc connectExecCmd(name string) *execProcess {\n\treturn &execProcess{sandboxName: name}\n}\n\ntype execProcess struct {\n\tsandboxName string\n}\n\nfunc (e *execProcess) Run() error {\n\t// Shell out to `sandcastle connect <name>`\n\t// We use the same binary so the user's config/auth is used\n\targs := []string{\"connect\", e.sandboxName}\n\tcmd := rootCmd\n\tcmd.SetArgs(args)\n\treturn cmd.Execute()\n}\n\n// ---------- entry point ----------\n\nfunc runTUI() error {\n\tclient, err := api.NewClient()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\n\tp := tea.NewProgram(newTUI(client), tea.WithAltScreen())\n\t_, err = p.Run()\n\treturn err\n}\n"
}

43	// ---------- views ----------
44	
45	type tuiView int
46	
47	const (
48		viewSandboxes tuiView = iota
49		viewRoutes
50		viewCreateSandbox
51		viewAddRoute
52		viewConfirmDelete
53	)

57	type sandboxesLoadedMsg struct {

60	}

77		view     tuiView
78		cursor   int
79		width    int
80		height   int
81		spinner  spinner.Model
82		loading  bool
83		feedback string
84		feedErr  bool
85	
86		// sandbox list
87		sandboxes []api.Sandbox
88

91		routes       []api.RouteResponse

106	func newTUI(client *api.Client) tuiModel {
107		s := spinner.New()
108		s.Spinner = spinner.Dot
109		s.Style = lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color("214"))
110	
111		ti := textinput.New()
112		ti.Placeholder = "sandbox name (leave empty for auto)"
113		ti.CharLimit = 40
114		ti.Width = 40
115	
116		domainInput := textinput.New()
117		domainInput.Placeholder = "domain (e.g. app.example.com)"
118		domainInput.CharLimit = 80
119		domainInput.Width = 40
120	
121		portInput := textinput.New()
122		portInput.Placeholder = "port (default 8080)"
123		portInput.CharLimit = 5
124		portInput.Width = 10
125	
126		return tuiModel{
127			client:      client,
128			spinner:     s,
129			loading:     true,
130			createInput: ti,
131			routeInputs: [2]textinput.Model{domainInput, portInput},
132		}
133	}
134	
135	func (m tuiModel) Init() tea.Cmd {
136		return tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, loadSandboxes(m.client))
137	}
138	
139	// ---------- commands ----------
140	
141	func loadSandboxes(client *api.Client) tea.Cmd {
142		return func() tea.Msg {
143			sandboxes, err := client.ListSandboxes()
144			return sandboxesLoadedMsg{sandboxes, err}
145		}
146	}
147	
148	func loadRoutes(client *api.Client, sandboxID int) tea.Cmd {
149		return func() tea.Msg {
150			routes, err := client.ListRoutes(sandboxID)
151			return routesLoadedMsg{routes, err}
152		}
153	}
154	
155	func doAction(fn func() (string, error)) tea.Cmd {
156		return func() tea.Msg {
157			msg, err := fn()
158			return actionDoneMsg{msg, err}
159		}
160	}
161	
162	// ---------- update ----------
163	
164	func (m tuiModel) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
165		switch msg := msg.(type) {
166		case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
167			m.width = msg.Width
168			m.height = msg.Height
169			return m, nil
170	
171		case spinner.TickMsg:
172			var cmd tea.Cmd
173			m.spinner, cmd = m.spinner.Update(msg)
174			return m, cmd
175	
176		case sandboxesLoadedMsg:
177			m.loading = false
178			if msg.err != nil {
179				m.feedback = msg.err.Error()
180				m.feedErr = true
181				return m, nil
182			}
183			m.sandboxes = msg.sandboxes
184			if m.cursor >= len(m.sandboxes) {
185				m.cursor = max(0, len(m.sandboxes)-1)
186			}
187			return m, nil
188	
189		case routesLoadedMsg:
190			m.loading = false
191			if msg.err != nil {
192				m.feedback = msg.err.Error()
193				m.feedErr = true
194				m.view = viewSandboxes
195				return m, nil
196			}
197			m.routes = msg.routes
198			m.routeCursor = 0
199			return m, nil
200	
201		case actionDoneMsg:
202			m.loading = false
203			if msg.err != nil {
204				m.feedback = msg.err.Error()
205				m.feedErr = true
206			} else {
207				m.feedback = msg.msg
208				m.feedErr = false
209			}
210			if m.view == viewConfirmDelete {
211				m.view = viewSandboxes
212			}
213			// Reload context-appropriate data
214			m.loading = true
215			if m.view == viewRoutes && m.routeSandbox != nil {
216				return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, loadRoutes(m.client, m.routeSandbox.ID))
217			}
218			return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, loadSandboxes(m.client))
219		}
220	
221		switch m.view {
222		case viewSandboxes:
223			return m.updateSandboxes(msg)
224		case viewRoutes:
225			return m.updateRoutes(msg)
226		case viewCreateSandbox:
227			return m.updateCreate(msg)
228		case viewAddRoute:
229			return m.updateAddRoute(msg)
230		case viewConfirmDelete:
231			return m.updateConfirmDelete(msg)
232		}
233		return m, nil
234	}
235	
236	func (m tuiModel) updateSandboxes(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
237		switch msg := msg.(type) {
238		case tea.KeyMsg:
239			m.feedback = ""
240			switch {
241			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("q", "ctrl+c"))):
242				return m, tea.Quit
243			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("up", "k"))):
244				if m.cursor > 0 {
245					m.cursor--
246				}
247			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("down", "j"))):
248				if m.cursor < len(m.sandboxes)-1 {
249					m.cursor++
250				}
251			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("c"))):
252				m.view = viewCreateSandbox
253				m.createInput.SetValue("")
254				m.createInput.Focus()
255				return m, m.createInput.Cursor.BlinkCmd()
256			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("r"))):
257				if len(m.sandboxes) > 0 {
258					m.loading = true
259					m.feedback = ""
260					sb := m.sandboxes[m.cursor]
261					m.routeSandbox = &sb
262					m.view = viewRoutes
263					return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, loadRoutes(m.client, sb.ID))
264				}
265			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("R"))):
266				m.loading = true
267				m.feedback = ""
268				return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, loadSandboxes(m.client))
269			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("s"))):
270				if len(m.sandboxes) > 0 {
271					sb := m.sandboxes[m.cursor]
272					if sb.Status == "stopped" {
273						m.loading = true
274						return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {
275							_, err := m.client.StartSandbox(sb.ID)
276							return fmt.Sprintf("%q started", sb.Name), err
277						}))
278					}
279				}
280			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("x"))):
281				if len(m.sandboxes) > 0 {
282					sb := m.sandboxes[m.cursor]
283					if sb.Status == "running" {
284						m.loading = true
285						return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {
286							_, err := m.client.StopSandbox(sb.ID)
287							return fmt.Sprintf("%q stopped", sb.Name), err
288						}))
289					}
290				}
291			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("d"))):
292				if len(m.sandboxes) > 0 {
293					sb := m.sandboxes[m.cursor]
294					m.deleteTarget = sb.Name
295					m.deleteID = sb.ID
296					m.view = viewConfirmDelete
297				}
298			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("enter"))):
299				// connect — exit TUI, then reconnect via the CLI connect command
300				if len(m.sandboxes) > 0 {
301					sb := m.sandboxes[m.cursor]
302					exe, _ := os.Executable()
303					c := exec.Command(exe, "connect", sb.Name)
304					c.Stdin = os.Stdin
305					c.Stdout = os.Stdout
306					c.Stderr = os.Stderr
307					return m, tea.ExecProcess(c, func(err error) tea.Msg {
308						return actionDoneMsg{"", err}
309					})
310				}
311			}
312		}
313		return m, nil
314	}
315	
316	func (m tuiModel) updateRoutes(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
317		switch msg := msg.(type) {
318		case tea.KeyMsg:
319			m.feedback = ""
320			switch {
321			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("q", "esc"))):
322				m.view = viewSandboxes
323				m.routes = nil
324				m.routeSandbox = nil
325			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("ctrl+c"))):
326				return m, tea.Quit
327			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("up", "k"))):
328				if m.routeCursor > 0 {
329					m.routeCursor--
330				}
331			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("down", "j"))):
332				if m.routeCursor < len(m.routes)-1 {
333					m.routeCursor++
334				}
335			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("a"))):
336				m.view = viewAddRoute
337				m.routeFocusIdx = 0
338				m.routeInputs[0].SetValue("")
339				m.routeInputs[1].SetValue("")
340				m.routeInputs[0].Focus()
341				m.routeInputs[1].Blur()
342				return m, m.routeInputs[0].Cursor.BlinkCmd()
343			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("d"))):
344				if len(m.routes) > 0 {
345					r := m.routes[m.routeCursor]
346					sbID := m.routeSandbox.ID
347					m.loading = true
348					return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {
349						err := m.client.RemoveRouteByID(sbID, r.ID)
350						return fmt.Sprintf("Route #%d removed", r.ID), err
351					}))
352				}
353			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("R"))):
354				if m.routeSandbox != nil {
355					m.loading = true
356					return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, loadRoutes(m.client, m.routeSandbox.ID))
357				}
358			}
359		}
360		return m, nil
361	}
362	
363	func (m tuiModel) updateCreate(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
364		switch msg := msg.(type) {
365		case tea.KeyMsg:
366			switch msg.Type {
367			case tea.KeyEsc:
368				m.view = viewSandboxes
369				return m, nil
370			case tea.KeyCtrlC:
371				return m, tea.Quit
372			case tea.KeyEnter:
373				name := strings.TrimSpace(m.createInput.Value())
374				if name == "" {
375					name = fmt.Sprintf("temp-%d", time.Now().Unix())
376				}
377				m.view = viewSandboxes
378				m.loading = true
379				return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {
380					sb, err := m.client.CreateSandbox(api.CreateSandboxRequest{
381						Name:          name,
382						Image:         "ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest",
383						VNCEnabled:    true,
384						DockerEnabled: true,
385					})
386					if err != nil {
387						return "", err
388					}
389					return fmt.Sprintf("%q created", sb.Name), nil
390				}))
391			}
392		}
393		var cmd tea.Cmd
394		m.createInput, cmd = m.createInput.Update(msg)
395		return m, cmd
396	}
397	
398	func (m tuiModel) updateAddRoute(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
399		switch msg := msg.(type) {
400		case tea.KeyMsg:
401			switch msg.Type {
402			case tea.KeyEsc:
403				m.view = viewRoutes
404				return m, nil
405			case tea.KeyCtrlC:
406				return m, tea.Quit
407			case tea.KeyTab, tea.KeyShiftTab:
408				m.routeFocusIdx = (m.routeFocusIdx + 1) % 2
409				for i := range m.routeInputs {
410					if i == m.routeFocusIdx {
411						m.routeInputs[i].Focus()
412					} else {
413						m.routeInputs[i].Blur()
414					}
415				}
416				return m, m.routeInputs[m.routeFocusIdx].Cursor.BlinkCmd()
417			case tea.KeyEnter:
418				domain := strings.TrimSpace(m.routeInputs[0].Value())
419				portStr := strings.TrimSpace(m.routeInputs[1].Value())
420				if domain == "" {
421					m.feedback = "domain is required"
422					m.feedErr = true
423					return m, nil
424				}
425				port := 8080
426				if portStr != "" {
427					n := 0
428					for _, ch := range portStr {
429						if ch < '0' || ch > '9' {
430							m.feedback = "invalid port"
431							m.feedErr = true
432							return m, nil
433						}
434						n = n*10 + int(ch-'0')
435					}
436					port = n
437				}
438				sbID := m.routeSandbox.ID
439				m.view = viewRoutes
440				m.loading = true
441				return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {
442					r, err := m.client.AddRoute(sbID, api.RouteRequest{
443						Domain: domain,
444						Port:   port,
445						Mode:   "http",
446					})
447					if err != nil {
448						return "", err
449					}
450					return fmt.Sprintf("Route added: %s → :%d", r.Domain, r.Port), nil
451				}))
452			}
453		}
454	
455		var cmd tea.Cmd
456		m.routeInputs[m.routeFocusIdx], cmd = m.routeInputs[m.routeFocusIdx].Update(msg)
457		return m, cmd
458	}
459	
460	func (m tuiModel) updateConfirmDelete(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
461		switch msg := msg.(type) {
462		case tea.KeyMsg:
463			switch {
464			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("y", "Y"))):
465				id := m.deleteID
466				name := m.deleteTarget
467				m.loading = true
468				return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {
469					err := m.client.DestroySandbox(id)
470					return fmt.Sprintf("%q destroyed", name), err
471				}))
472			default:
473				m.view = viewSandboxes
474				m.feedback = "cancelled"
475				m.feedErr = false
476			}
477		}
478		return m, nil
479	}

481	// ---------- view ----------
482	
483	func (m tuiModel) View() string {
484		var b strings.Builder
485	
486		title := titleStyle.Render("  Sandcastle")
487		if m.client.ServerAlias != "" {
488			title += helpStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf("  %s", m.client.ServerAlias))
489		}
490		b.WriteString(title + "\n\n")
491	
492		switch m.view {
493		case viewSandboxes:
494			m.viewSandboxes(&b)
495		case viewRoutes:
496			m.viewRoutes(&b)
497		case viewCreateSandbox:
498			m.viewCreate(&b)
499		case viewAddRoute:
500			m.viewAddRoute(&b)
501		case viewConfirmDelete:
502			m.viewConfirmDelete(&b)
503		}
504	
505		// Feedback
506		if m.feedback != "" {
507			b.WriteString("\n")
508			if m.feedErr {
509				b.WriteString(errStyle.Render("  " + m.feedback))
510			} else {
511				b.WriteString(okStyle.Render("  " + m.feedback))
512			}
513			b.WriteString("\n")
514		}
515	
516		return b.String()
517	}
518	
519	func (m tuiModel) viewSandboxes(b *strings.Builder) {
520		if m.loading && len(m.sandboxes) == 0 {
521			b.WriteString("  " + m.spinner.View() + " Loading sandboxes...\n")
522			b.WriteString("\n" + helpStyle.Render("  q quit"))
523			return
524		}
525	
526		if len(m.sandboxes) == 0 {
527			b.WriteString("  No sandboxes. Press c to create one.\n")
528		} else {
529			// Header
530			b.WriteString(headerStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf("  %-22s %-10s %-18s %s", "NAME", "STATUS", "CREATED", "ROUTE")) + "\n")
531	
532			for i, sb := range m.sandboxes {
533				name := sb.Name
534				if sb.Temporary {
535					name += " ~"
536				}
537				if len(name) > 22 {
538					name = name[:21] + "…"
539				}
540	
541				st := statusStopped.Render(sb.Status)
542				if sb.Status == "running" {
543					st = statusRunning.Render(sb.Status)
544				}
545	
546				created := sb.CreatedAt.Local().Format("Jan 02 15:04")
547	
548				route := ""
549				if len(sb.Routes) > 0 {
550					parts := make([]string, 0, len(sb.Routes))
551					for _, r := range sb.Routes {
552						if r.Mode == "tcp" {
553							parts = append(parts, fmt.Sprintf("tcp/:%d", r.PublicPort))
554						} else {
555							parts = append(parts, r.Domain)
556						}
557					}
558					route = strings.Join(parts, ", ")
559					if len(route) > 30 {
560						route = route[:29] + "…"
561					}
562				}
563	
564				line := fmt.Sprintf("  %-22s %-10s %-18s %s", name, st, created, route)
565				if i == m.cursor {
566					// Re-render with selection styling — pad to width
567					padded := fmt.Sprintf("  %-22s %-20s %-18s %-30s", name, sb.Status, created, route)
568					line = selectedStyle.Render(padded)
569				}
570				b.WriteString(line + "\n")
571			}
572		}
573	
574		if m.loading {
575			b.WriteString("\n  " + m.spinner.View() + "\n")
576		}
577	
578		b.WriteString("\n")
579		b.WriteString(helpStyle.Render("  enter connect  c create  s start  x stop  d destroy  r routes  R refresh  q quit"))
580		b.WriteString("\n")
581	}
582	
583	func (m tuiModel) viewRoutes(b *strings.Builder) {
584		b.WriteString(headerStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf("  Routes for %s", m.routeSandbox.Name)) + "\n\n")
585	
586		if m.loading {
587			b.WriteString("  " + m.spinner.View() + " Loading routes...\n")
588			b.WriteString("\n" + helpStyle.Render("  esc back  q quit"))
589			return
590		}
591	
592		if len(m.routes) == 0 {
593			b.WriteString("  No routes. Press a to add one.\n")
594		} else {
595			b.WriteString(headerStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf("  %-6s %-6s %-30s %-8s %s", "ID", "MODE", "DOMAIN / PUBLIC PORT", "PORT", "URL")) + "\n")
596			for i, r := range m.routes {
597				target := r.Domain
598				if r.Mode == "tcp" {
599					target = fmt.Sprintf(":%d", r.PublicPort)
600				}
601				url := r.URL
602				if len(url) > 40 {
603					url = url[:39] + "…"
604				}
605				line := fmt.Sprintf("  %-6d %-6s %-30s %-8d %s", r.ID, r.Mode, target, r.Port, url)
606				if i == m.routeCursor {
607					line = selectedStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf("  %-6d %-6s %-30s %-8d %-40s", r.ID, r.Mode, target, r.Port, url))
608				}
609				b.WriteString(line + "\n")
610			}
611		}
612	
613		b.WriteString("\n")
614		b.WriteString(helpStyle.Render("  a add  d delete  R refresh  esc/q back"))
615		b.WriteString("\n")
616	}
617	
618	func (m tuiModel) viewCreate(b *strings.Builder) {
619		b.WriteString(headerStyle.Render("  Create Sandbox") + "\n\n")
620		b.WriteString("  Name: " + m.createInput.View() + "\n")
621		b.WriteString("\n")
622		b.WriteString(helpStyle.Render("  enter confirm  esc cancel"))
623		b.WriteString("\n")
624	}
625	
626	func (m tuiModel) viewAddRoute(b *strings.Builder) {
627		b.WriteString(headerStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf("  Add Route to %s", m.routeSandbox.Name)) + "\n\n")
628		b.WriteString("  Domain: " + m.routeInputs[0].View() + "\n")
629		b.WriteString("  Port:   " + m.routeInputs[1].View() + "\n")
630		b.WriteString("\n")
631		b.WriteString(helpStyle.Render("  tab next field  enter confirm  esc cancel"))
632		b.WriteString("\n")
633	}
634	
635	func (m tuiModel) viewConfirmDelete(b *strings.Builder) {
636		b.WriteString(errStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf("  Destroy sandbox %q?", m.deleteTarget)) + "\n\n")
637		b.WriteString("  Press y to confirm, any other key to cancel.\n")
638	}
639	
640	// ---------- entry point ----------
641	
642	func runTUI() error {
643		client, err := api.NewClient()
644		if err != nil {
645			return err
646		}
647	
648		p := tea.NewProgram(newTUI(client), tea.WithAltScreen())
649		_, err = p.Run()
650		return err
651	}
652	
```

> AGENT

The TUI is ready. Here's what was done:

**New file:** `cmd/tui.go` — a Bubble Tea TUI with 5 views:

| View | Description |
|------|-------------|
| **Sandboxes** | Main list with name, status, created date, routes |
| **Routes** | Route list for selected sandbox |
| **Create** | Text input for new sandbox name |
| **Add Route** | Domain + port inputs |
| **Confirm Delete** | y/n confirmation |

**Keybindings (sandbox list):**
- `j/k` or arrows — navigate
- `enter` — connect (drops into SSH via `sandcastle connect`)
- `c` — create sandbox
- `s` — start stopped sandbox
- `x` — stop running sandbox
- `d` — destroy (with confirmation)
- `r` — manage routes for selected sandbox
- `R` — refresh
- `q` — quit

**Keybindings (routes view):**
- `a` — add HTTP route (domain + port)
- `d` — delete selected route
- `esc/q` — back to sandbox list

**Modified:** `cmd/root.go` — added `RunE` to root command so `sandcastle` with no args launches the TUI.

**Dependencies added:** `charmbracelet/bubbletea`, `charmbracelet/lipgloss`, `charmbracelet/bubbles`

1	package config
2	
3	import (
4		"fmt"
5		"os"

8	
9		"gopkg.in/yaml.v3"
