11	
12	type ServerConfig struct {
13		URL      string `yaml:"url"`
14		Token    string `yaml:"token"`
15		Insecure bool   `yaml:"insecure,omitempty"`
16	}
17	
18	// Preferences holds user-configurable CLI behaviour. Each field can be
19	// overridden by a SANDCASTLE_<KEY> environment variable at runtime.
20	type Preferences struct {
21		ConnectProtocol string `yaml:"connect_protocol,omitempty"` // "ssh" (default) | "mosh"
22		UseTmux         *bool  `yaml:"use_tmux,omitempty"`         // default true
23		SSHExtraArgs    string `yaml:"ssh_extra_args,omitempty"`   // extra flags for ssh/mosh
24		MountHome       *bool  `yaml:"mount_home,omitempty"`       // default false; --home on create
25		DataPath        string `yaml:"data_path,omitempty"`        // default ""; --data on create
26		VNC             *bool  `yaml:"vnc,omitempty"`              // default true; false → --no-vnc on create
27		Docker          *bool  `yaml:"docker,omitempty"`           // default true; false → --no-docker on create
28	}
29	
30	type Config struct {
31		CurrentServer string                  `yaml:"current_server"`
32		Servers       map[string]ServerConfig `yaml:"servers"`
33		Preferences   Preferences             `yaml:"preferences,omitempty"`
34	}
35	
36	// legacyConfig is the old flat format for migration.
37	type legacyConfig struct {
38		Server string `yaml:"server"`
39		Token  string `yaml:"token"`
40	}
41	
42	func Dir() string {
43		home, _ := os.UserHomeDir()
44		return filepath.Join(home, ".sandcastle")
45	}
46	
47	func Path() string {
48		return filepath.Join(Dir(), "config.yaml")
49	}
50	
51	func Load() (*Config, error) {
52		cfg := &Config{Servers: make(map[string]ServerConfig)}
53		data, err := os.ReadFile(Path())
54		if err != nil {
55			if os.IsNotExist(err) {
56				return cfg, nil
57			}
58			return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading config: %w", err)
59		}
60	
61		// Try new format first
62		if err := yaml.Unmarshal(data, cfg); err != nil {
63			return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing config: %w", err)
64		}
65	
66		// Migrate legacy flat format
67		if cfg.Servers == nil || len(cfg.Servers) == 0 {
68			var legacy legacyConfig
69			if err := yaml.Unmarshal(data, &legacy); err == nil && legacy.Server != "" {
70				cfg.Servers = map[string]ServerConfig{
71					"default": {URL: legacy.Server, Token: legacy.Token},
72				}
73				cfg.CurrentServer = "default"
74				// Save migrated config
75				_ = Save(cfg)
76			}
77		}
78	
79		if cfg.Servers == nil {
80			cfg.Servers = make(map[string]ServerConfig)
81		}
82	
83		return cfg, nil
84	}
85	
86	func Save(cfg *Config) error {
87		if err := os.MkdirAll(Dir(), 0o700); err != nil {
88			return fmt.Errorf("creating config dir: %w", err)
89		}
90		data, err := yaml.Marshal(cfg)
91		if err != nil {
92			return fmt.Errorf("marshaling config: %w", err)
93		}
94		if err := os.WriteFile(Path(), data, 0o600); err != nil {
95			return fmt.Errorf("writing config: %w", err)
96		}
97		return nil
98	}
99	
100	// CurrentServerConfig returns the active server's URL and token.
101	func (c *Config) CurrentServerConfig() (ServerConfig, error) {
102		if c.CurrentServer == "" {
103			return ServerConfig{}, fmt.Errorf("no server configured — run: sandcastle login <url>")
104		}
105		srv, ok := c.Servers[c.CurrentServer]
106		if !ok {
107			return ServerConfig{}, fmt.Errorf("server %q not found in config — run: sandcastle login <url>", c.CurrentServer)
108		}
109		return srv, nil
110	}
111	
112	// SetServer adds or updates a server and sets it as current.
113	func (c *Config) SetServer(alias, url, token string, insecure bool) {
114		if c.Servers == nil {
115			c.Servers = make(map[string]ServerConfig)
116		}
117		c.Servers[alias] = ServerConfig{URL: url, Token: token, Insecure: insecure}
118		c.CurrentServer = alias
119	}
120	
121	// LoadPreferences returns effective preferences with env vars overlaid.
122	// Priority: ENV var > config file preference > built-in default.
123	func (c *Config) LoadPreferences() Preferences {
124		p := c.Preferences
125	
126		if v := os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_CONNECT_PROTOCOL"); v != "" {
127			p.ConnectProtocol = v
128		}
129		if v := os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_USE_TMUX"); v != "" {
130			b := strings.ToLower(v) == "true" || v == "1"
131			p.UseTmux = &b
132		}
133		if v := os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_SSH_EXTRA_ARGS"); v != "" {
134			p.SSHExtraArgs = v
135		}
136		if v := os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_HOME"); v != "" {
137			b := strings.ToLower(v) == "true" || v == "1"
138			p.MountHome = &b
139		}
140		if v := os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_DATA"); v != "" {
141			if v == "1" || v == "true" {
142				v = "."
143			}
144			p.DataPath = v
145		}
146		if v := os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_VNC"); v != "" {
147			b := strings.ToLower(v) == "true" || v == "1"
148			p.VNC = &b
149		}
150		if v := os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_DOCKER"); v != "" {
151			b := strings.ToLower(v) == "true" || v == "1"
152			p.Docker = &b
153		}
154	
155		// Apply built-in defaults
156		if p.ConnectProtocol == "" {
157			p.ConnectProtocol = "ssh"
158		}
159		if p.UseTmux == nil {
160			t := true
161			p.UseTmux = &t
162		}
163	
164		return p
165	}
166	
167	// SetPreference sets a named preference by string value and validates it.
168	func (c *Config) SetPreference(key, value string) error {
169		switch key {
170		case "connect_protocol":
171			if value != "ssh" && value != "mosh" && value != "auto" {
172				return fmt.Errorf("connect_protocol must be 'ssh', 'mosh', or 'auto', got %q", value)
173			}
174			if value == "auto" {
175				c.Preferences.ConnectProtocol = ""
176			} else {
177				c.Preferences.ConnectProtocol = value
178			}
179		case "use_tmux":
180			switch strings.ToLower(value) {
181			case "true", "1", "yes":
182				t := true
183				c.Preferences.UseTmux = &t
184			case "false", "0", "no":
185				f := false
186				c.Preferences.UseTmux = &f
187			default:
188				return fmt.Errorf("use_tmux must be 'true' or 'false', got %q", value)
189			}
190		case "ssh_extra_args":
191			c.Preferences.SSHExtraArgs = value
192		case "mount_home":
193			switch strings.ToLower(value) {
194			case "true", "1", "yes":
195				t := true
196				c.Preferences.MountHome = &t
197			case "false", "0", "no":
198				f := false
199				c.Preferences.MountHome = &f
200			default:
201				return fmt.Errorf("mount_home must be 'true' or 'false', got %q", value)
202			}
203		case "data_path":
204			if value == "false" || value == "0" || value == "no" || value == "off" {
205				c.Preferences.DataPath = ""
206			} else {
207				if value == "true" || value == "1" {
208					value = "."
209				}
210				c.Preferences.DataPath = value
211			}
212		case "vnc":
213			switch strings.ToLower(value) {
214			case "true", "1", "yes":
215				t := true
216				c.Preferences.VNC = &t
217			case "false", "0", "no":
218				f := false
219				c.Preferences.VNC = &f
220			default:
221				return fmt.Errorf("vnc must be 'true' or 'false', got %q", value)
222			}
223		case "docker":
224			switch strings.ToLower(value) {
225			case "true", "1", "yes":
226				t := true
227				c.Preferences.Docker = &t
228			case "false", "0", "no":
229				f := false
230				c.Preferences.Docker = &f
231			default:
232				return fmt.Errorf("docker must be 'true' or 'false', got %q", value)
233			}
234		default:
235			return fmt.Errorf("unknown preference %q; valid keys: connect_protocol, use_tmux, ssh_extra_args, mount_home, data_path, vnc, docker", key)
236		}
237		return nil
238	}

fieldText {\n\t\t\tm.createFields[i].input.Blur()\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tm.createCursor = idx\n\tif m.createFields[idx].kind == fieldText {\n\t\tm.createFields[idx].input.Focus()\n\t\treturn m, m.createFields[idx].input.Cursor.BlinkCmd()\n\t}\n\treturn m, nil\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) updateCreate(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tf := &m.createFields[m.createCursor]\n\n\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n\tcase tea.KeyMsg:\n\t\tswitch msg.Type {\n\t\tcase tea.KeyEsc:\n\t\t\tm.view = viewSandboxes\n\t\t\tm.feedback = \"\"\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\tcase tea.KeyCtrlC:\n\t\t\treturn m, tea.Quit\n\t\tcase tea.KeyUp:\n\t\t\tif m.createCursor > 0 {\n\t\t\t\treturn m.createFocusField(m.createCursor - 1)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\tcase tea.KeyDown, tea.KeyTab:\n\t\t\tif m.createCursor < len(m.createFields)-1 {\n\t\t\t\treturn m.createFocusField(m.createCursor + 1)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\tcase tea.KeyShiftTab:\n\t\t\tif m.createCursor > 0 {\n\t\t\t\treturn m.createFocusField(m.createCursor - 1)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\tcase tea.KeyEnter:\n\t\t\tif f.kind == fieldBool {\n\t\t\t\t// Toggle on enter too\n\t\t\t\tf.boolVal = !f.boolVal\n\t\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// Submit the form if on last field or ctrl+enter intent\n\t\t\treturn m.submitCreateForm()\n\t\tcase tea.KeyCtrlS:\n\t\t\treturn m.submitCreateForm()\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Space toggles booleans\n\t\tif f.kind == fieldBool && msg.String() == \" \" {\n\t\t\tf.boolVal = !f.boolVal\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Tab-completion for snapshot field\n\t\tif m.createCursor == cfSnapshot && f.kind == fieldText && msg.String() == \"right\" {\n\t\t\tm = m.completeSnapshot()\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Forward to text input if current field is text\n\tif f.kind == fieldText {\n\t\tvar cmd tea.Cmd\n\t\tf.input, cmd = f.input.Update(msg)\n\t\treturn m, cmd\n\t}\n\treturn m, nil\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) completeSnapshot() tuiModel {\n\tif len(m.snapshots) == 0 {\n\t\treturn m\n\t}\n\tcurrent := strings.ToLower(m.createFields[cfSnapshot].input.Value())\n\tfor _, snap := range m.snapshots {\n\t\tif current == \"\" || strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(snap.Name), current) {\n\t\t\tm.createFields[cfSnapshot].input.SetValue(snap.Name)\n\t\t\tm.createFields[cfSnapshot].input.CursorEnd()\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn m\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) submitCreateForm() (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tname := strings.TrimSpace(m.createFields[cfName].input.Value())\n\tif name == \"\" {\n\t\tname = fmt.Sprintf(\"temp-%d\", time.Now().Unix())\n\t}\n\timage := strings.TrimSpace(m.createFields[cfImage].input.Value())\n\tif image == \"\" {\n\t\timage = \"ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest\"\n\t}\n\tsnapshot := strings.TrimSpace(m.createFields[cfSnapshot].input.Value())\n\tdataPath := strings.TrimSpace(m.createFields[cfData].input.Value())\n\n\treq := api.CreateSandboxRequest{\n\t\tName:          name,\n\t\tImage:         image,\n\t\tFromSnapshot:  snapshot,\n\t\tDockerEnabled: m.createFields[cfDocker].boolVal,\n\t\tVNCEnabled:    m.createFields[cfVNC].boolVal,\n\t\tTailscale:     m.createFields[cfTailscale].boolVal,\n\t\tMountHome:     m.createFields[cfHome].boolVal,\n\t\tDataPath:      dataPath,\n\t\tSMBEnabled:    m.createFields[cfSMB].boolVal,\n\t\tTemporary:     m.createFields[cfTemporary].boolVal,\n\t}\n\n\tm.view = viewSandboxes\n\tm.loading = true\n\treturn m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {\n\t\tsb, err := 45	type tuiView int

55	// ---------- form field types ----------

70	}

77	}
78

85		snapshots []api.Snapshot
86		err       error
87	}

90		msg string
91		err error
92	}
93	
94	// ---------- model ----------
95	
96	type tuiModel struct {
97		client *api.Client
98	
99		view     tuiView
100		cursor   int
101		width    int
102		height   int
103		spinner  spinner.Model
104		loading  bool
105		feedback string
106		feedErr  bool
107	
108		// sandbox list
109		sandboxes []api.Sandbox
110

113		routes       []api.RouteResponse
114		routeCursor  int
115	
116		// create sandbox form
117		createFields   []formField
118		createCursor   int

120	
121		// add route

123		routeFocusIdx  int

129	
130	func makeTextInput(placeholder string, width int) textinput.Model {
131		ti := textinput.New()
132		ti.Placeholder = placeholder
133		ti.CharLimit = 80
134		ti.Width = width
135		return ti
136	}
137	
138	// Create form field indices — keep in sync with buildCreateFields.
139	const (
140		cfName = iota
141		cfImage
142		cfSnapshot
143		cfDocker
144		cfVNC
145		cfTailscale
146		cfHome
147		cfData
148		cfSMB
149		cfTemporary
150		cfCount
151	)
152	
153	func buildCreateFields() []formField {
154		return []formField{
155			{label: "Name", kind: fieldText, input: makeTextInput("leave empty for auto-generated name", 45)},
156			{label: "Image", kind: fieldText, input: makeTextInput("ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest", 55)},
157			{label: "Snapshot", kind: fieldText, input: makeTextInput("snapshot name to restore from", 45)},
158			{label: "Docker", kind: fieldBool, boolVal: true, defBool: true},
159			{label: "VNC", kind: fieldBool, boolVal: true, defBool: true},
160			{label: "Tailscale", kind: fieldBool, boolVal: false, defBool: false},
161			{label: "Home", kind: fieldBool, boolVal: false, defBool: false},
162			{label: "Data path", kind: fieldText, input: makeTextInput("subpath or . for root (empty = none)", 45)},
163			{label: "SMB", kind: fieldBool, boolVal: false, defBool: false},
164			{label: "Temporary", kind: fieldBool, boolVal: false, defBool: false},
165		}
166	}
167	
168	func newTUI(client *api.Client) tuiModel {
169		s := spinner.New()
170		s.Spinner = spinner.Dot
171		s.Style = lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color("214"))
172	
173		domainInput := makeTextInput("domain (e.g. app.example.com)", 40)
174		portInput := makeTextInput("port (default 8080)", 10)
175		portInput.CharLimit = 5
176	
177		return tuiModel{
178			client:       client,
179			spinner:      s,
180			loading:      true,
181			createFields: buildCreateFields(),
182			routeInputs:  [2]textinput.Model{domainInput, portInput},
183		}
184	}
185	
186	func (m tuiModel) Init() tea.Cmd {
187		return tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, loadSandboxes(m.client))
188	}
189	
190	// ---------- commands ----------
191	
192	func loadSandboxes(client *api.Client) tea.Cmd {
193		return func() tea.Msg {
194			sandboxes, err := client.ListSandboxes()
195			return sandboxesLoadedMsg{sandboxes, err}
196		}
197	}
198	
199	func loadRoutes(client *api.Client, sandboxID int) tea.Cmd {
200		return func() tea.Msg {
201			routes, err := client.ListRoutes(sandboxID)
202			return routesLoadedMsg{routes, err}
203		}
204	}
205	
206	func loadSnapshots(client *api.Client) tea.Cmd {
207		return func() tea.Msg {
208			snapshots, err := client.ListSnapshots()
209			return snapshotsLoadedMsg{snapshots, err}
210		}
211	}
212	
213	func doAction(fn func() (string, error)) tea.Cmd {
214		return func() tea.Msg {
215			msg, err := fn()
216			return actionDoneMsg{msg, err}
217		}
218	}
219	
220	// ---------- update ----------
221	
222	func (m tuiModel) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
223		switch msg := msg.(type) {
224		case tea.WindowSizeMsg:
225			m.width = msg.Width
226			m.height = msg.Height
227			return m, nil
228	
229		case spinner.TickMsg:
230			var cmd tea.Cmd
231			m.spinner, cmd = m.spinner.Update(msg)
232			return m, cmd
233	
234		case sandboxesLoadedMsg:
235			m.loading = false
236			if msg.err != nil {
237				m.feedback = msg.err.Error()
238				m.feedErr = true
239				return m, nil
240			}
241			m.sandboxes = msg.sandboxes
242			if m.cursor >= len(m.sandboxes) {
243				m.cursor = max(0, len(m.sandboxes)-1)
244			}
245			return m, nil
246	
247		case snapshotsLoadedMsg:
248			if msg.err == nil {
249				m.snapshots = msg.snapshots
250			}
251			return m, nil
252	
253		case routesLoadedMsg:
254			m.loading = false
255			if msg.err != nil {
256				m.feedback = msg.err.Error()
257				m.feedErr = true
258				m.view = viewSandboxes
259				return m, nil
260			}
261			m.routes = msg.routes
262			m.routeCursor = 0
263			return m, nil
264	
265		case actionDoneMsg:
266			m.loading = false
267			if msg.err != nil {
268				m.feedback = msg.err.Error()
269				m.feedErr = true
270			} else {
271				m.feedback = msg.msg
272				m.feedErr = false
273			}
274			if m.view == viewConfirmDelete {
275				m.view = viewSandboxes
276			}
277			// Reload context-appropriate data
278			m.loading = true
279			if m.view == viewRoutes && m.routeSandbox != nil {
280				return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, loadRoutes(m.client, m.routeSandbox.ID))
281			}
282			return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, loadSandboxes(m.client))
283		}
284	
285		switch m.view {
286		case viewSandboxes:
287			return m.updateSandboxes(msg)
288		case viewRoutes:
289			return m.updateRoutes(msg)
290		case viewCreateSandbox:
291			return m.updateCreate(msg)
292		case viewAddRoute:
293			return m.updateAddRoute(msg)
294		case viewConfirmDelete:
295			return m.updateConfirmDelete(msg)
296		}
297		return m, nil
298	}
299	
300	func (m tuiModel) updateSandboxes(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
301		switch msg := msg.(type) {
302		case tea.KeyMsg:
303			m.feedback = ""
304			switch {
305			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("q", "ctrl+c"))):
306				return m, tea.Quit
307			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("up", "k"))):
308				if m.cursor > 0 {
309					m.cursor--
310				}
311			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("down", "j"))):
312				if m.cursor < len(m.sandboxes)-1 {
313					m.cursor++
314				}
315			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("c"))):
316				m.view = viewCreateSandbox
317				m.createFields = buildCreateFields()
318				m.createCursor = 0
319				m.createFields[0].input.Focus()
320				cmds := []tea.Cmd{m.createFields[0].input.Cursor.BlinkCmd()}
321				if len(m.snapshots) == 0 {
322					cmds = append(cmds, loadSnapshots(m.client))
323				}
324				return m, tea.Batch(cmds...)
325			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("r"))):
326				if len(m.sandboxes) > 0 {
327					m.loading = true
328					m.feedback = ""
329					sb := m.sandboxes[m.cursor]
330					m.routeSandbox = &sb
331					m.view = viewRoutes
332					return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, loadRoutes(m.client, sb.ID))
333				}
334			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("R"))):
335				m.loading = true
336				m.feedback = ""
337				return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, loadSandboxes(m.client))
338			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("s"))):
339				if len(m.sandboxes) > 0 {
340					sb := m.sandboxes[m.cursor]
341					if sb.Status == "stopped" {
342						m.loading = true
343						return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {
344							_, err := m.client.StartSandbox(sb.ID)
345							return fmt.Sprintf("%q started", sb.Name), err
346						}))
347					}
348				}
349			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("x"))):
350				if len(m.sandboxes) > 0 {
351					sb := m.sandboxes[m.cursor]
352					if sb.Status == "running" {
353						m.loading = true
354						return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {
355							_, err := m.client.StopSandbox(sb.ID)
356							return fmt.Sprintf("%q stopped", sb.Name), err
357						}))
358					}
359				}
360			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("d"))):
361				if len(m.sandboxes) > 0 {
362					sb := m.sandboxes[m.cursor]
363					m.deleteTarget = sb.Name
364					m.deleteID = sb.ID
365					m.view = viewConfirmDelete
366				}
367			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("enter"))):
368				// connect — exit TUI, then reconnect via the CLI connect command
369				if len(m.sandboxes) > 0 {
370					sb := m.sandboxes[m.cursor]
371					exe, _ := os.Executable()
372					c := exec.Command(exe, "connect", sb.Name)
373					c.Stdin = os.Stdin
374					c.Stdout = os.Stdout
375					c.Stderr = os.Stderr
376					return m, tea.ExecProcess(c, func(err error) tea.Msg {
377						return actionDoneMsg{"", err}
378					})
379				}
380			}
381		}
382		return m, nil
383	}
384	
385	func (m tuiModel) updateRoutes(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
386		switch msg := msg.(type) {
387		case tea.KeyMsg:
388			m.feedback = ""
389			switch {
390			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("q", "esc"))):
391				m.view = viewSandboxes
392				m.routes = nil
393				m.routeSandbox = nil
394			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("ctrl+c"))):
395				return m, tea.Quit
396			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("up", "k"))):
397				if m.routeCursor > 0 {
398					m.routeCursor--
399				}
400			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("down", "j"))):
401				if m.routeCursor < len(m.routes)-1 {
402					m.routeCursor++
403				}
404			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("a"))):
405				m.view = viewAddRoute
406				m.routeFocusIdx = 0
407				m.routeInputs[0].SetValue("")
408				m.routeInputs[1].SetValue("")
409				m.routeInputs[0].Focus()
410				m.routeInputs[1].Blur()
411				return m, m.routeInputs[0].Cursor.BlinkCmd()
412			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("d"))):
413				if len(m.routes) > 0 {
414					r := m.routes[m.routeCursor]
415					sbID := m.routeSandbox.ID
416					m.loading = true
417					return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {
418						err := m.client.RemoveRouteByID(sbID, r.ID)
419						return fmt.Sprintf("Route #%d removed", r.ID), err
420					}))
421				}
422			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("R"))):
423				if m.routeSandbox != nil {
424					m.loading = true
425					return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, loadRoutes(m.client, m.routeSandbox.ID))
426				}
427			}
428		}
429		return m, nil
430	}
431	
432	func (m tuiModel) createFocusField(idx int) (tuiModel, tea.Cmd) {
433		// Blur all text fields, focus the target one
434		for i := range m.createFields {
435			if m.createFields[i].kind == fieldText {
436				m.createFields[i].input.Blur()
437			}
438		}
439		m.createCursor = idx
440		if m.createFields[idx].kind == fieldText {
441			m.createFields[idx].input.Focus()
442			return m, m.createFields[idx].input.Cursor.BlinkCmd()
443		}
444		return m, nil
445	}
446	
447	func (m tuiModel) updateCreate(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
448		f := &m.createFields[m.createCursor]
449	
450		switch msg := msg.(type) {
451		case tea.KeyMsg:
452			switch msg.Type {
453			case tea.KeyEsc:
454				m.view = viewSandboxes
455				m.feedback = ""
456				return m, nil
457			case tea.KeyCtrlC:
458				return m, tea.Quit
459			case tea.KeyUp:
460				if m.createCursor > 0 {
461					return m.createFocusField(m.createCursor - 1)
462				}
463				return m, nil
464			case tea.KeyDown, tea.KeyTab:
465				if m.createCursor < len(m.createFields)-1 {
466					return m.createFocusField(m.createCursor + 1)
467				}
468				return m, nil
469			case tea.KeyShiftTab:
470				if m.createCursor > 0 {
471					return m.createFocusField(m.createCursor - 1)
472				}
473				return m, nil
474			case tea.KeyEnter:
475				if f.kind == fieldBool {
476					// Toggle on enter too
477					f.boolVal = !f.boolVal
478					return m, nil
479				}
480				// Submit the form if on last field or ctrl+enter intent
481				return m.submitCreateForm()
482			case tea.KeyCtrlS:
483				return m.submitCreateForm()
484			}
485	
486			// Space toggles booleans
487			if f.kind == fieldBool && msg.String() == " " {
488				f.boolVal = !f.boolVal
489				return m, nil
490			}
491	
492			// Tab-completion for snapshot field
493			if m.createCursor == cfSnapshot && f.kind == fieldText && msg.String() == "right" {
494				m = m.completeSnapshot()
495				return m, nil
496			}
497		}
498	
499		// Forward to text input if current field is text
500		if f.kind == fieldText {
501			var cmd tea.Cmd
502			f.input, cmd = f.input.Update(msg)
503			return m, cmd
504		}
505		return m, nil
506	}
507	
508	func (m tuiModel) completeSnapshot() tuiModel {
509		if len(m.snapshots) == 0 {
510			return m
511		}
512		current := strings.ToLower(m.createFields[cfSnapshot].input.Value())
513		for _, snap := range m.snapshots {
514			if current == "" || strings.HasPrefix(strings.ToLower(snap.Name), current) {
515				m.createFields[cfSnapshot].input.SetValue(snap.Name)
516				m.createFields[cfSnapshot].input.CursorEnd()
517				break
518			}
519		}
520		return m
521	}
522	
523	func (m tuiModel) submitCreateForm() (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
524		name := strings.TrimSpace(m.createFields[cfName].input.Value())
525		if name == "" {
526			name = fmt.Sprintf("temp-%d", time.Now().Unix())
527		}
528		image := strings.TrimSpace(m.createFields[cfImage].input.Value())
529		if image == "" {
530			image = "ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest"
531		}
532		snapshot := strings.TrimSpace(m.createFields[cfSnapshot].input.Value())
533		dataPath := strings.TrimSpace(m.createFields[cfData].input.Value())
534	
535		req := api.CreateSandboxRequest{
536			Name:          name,
537			Image:         image,
538			FromSnapshot:  snapshot,
539			DockerEnabled: m.createFields[cfDocker].boolVal,
540			VNCEnabled:    m.createFields[cfVNC].boolVal,
541			Tailscale:     m.createFields[cfTailscale].boolVal,
542			MountHome:     m.createFields[cfHome].boolVal,
543			DataPath:      dataPath,
544			SMBEnabled:    m.createFields[cfSMB].boolVal,
545			Temporary:     m.createFields[cfTemporary].boolVal,
546		}
547	
548		m.view = viewSandboxes
549		m.loading = true
550		return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {
551			sb, err := m.client.CreateSandbox(req)
552			if err != nil {
553				return "", err
554			}
555			return fmt.Sprintf("%q created", sb.Name), nil
556		}))
557	}
558	
559	func (m tuiModel) updateAddRoute(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
560		switch msg := msg.(type) {
561		case tea.KeyMsg:
562			switch msg.Type {
563			case tea.KeyEsc:
564				m.view = viewRoutes
565				return m, nil
566			case tea.KeyCtrlC:
567				return m, tea.Quit
568			case tea.KeyTab, tea.KeyShiftTab:
569				m.routeFocusIdx = (m.routeFocusIdx + 1) % 2
570				for i := range m.routeInputs {
571					if i == m.routeFocusIdx {
572						m.routeInputs[i].Focus()
573					} else {
574						m.routeInputs[i].Blur()
575					}
576				}
577				return m, m.routeInputs[m.routeFocusIdx].Cursor.BlinkCmd()
578			case tea.KeyEnter:
579				domain := strings.TrimSpace(m.routeInputs[0].Value())
580				portStr := strings.TrimSpace(m.routeInputs[1].Value())
581				if domain == "" {
582					m.feedback = "domain is required"
583					m.feedErr = true
584					return m, nil
585				}
586				port := 8080
587				if portStr != "" {
588					n := 0
589					for _, ch := range portStr {
590						if ch < '0' || ch > '9' {
591							m.feedback = "invalid port"
592							m.feedErr = true
593							return m, nil
594						}
595						n = n*10 + int(ch-'0')
596					}
597					port = n
598				}
599				sbID := m.routeSandbox.ID
600				m.view = viewRoutes
601				m.loading = true
602				return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {
603					r, err := m.client.AddRoute(sbID, api.RouteRequest{
604						Domain: domain,
605						Port:   port,
606						Mode:   "http",
607					})
608					if err != nil {
609						return "", err
610					}
611					return fmt.Sprintf("Route added: %s → :%d", r.Domain, r.Port), nil
612				}))
613			}
614		}
615	
616		var cmd tea.Cmd
617		m.routeInputs[m.routeFocusIdx], cmd = m.routeInputs[m.routeFocusIdx].Update(msg)
618		return m, cmd
619	}
620	
621	func (m tuiModel) updateConfirmDelete(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
622		switch msg := msg.(type) {
623		case tea.KeyMsg:
624			switch {
625			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("y", "Y"))):
626				id := m.deleteID
627				name := m.deleteTarget
628				m.loading = true
629				return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, doAction(func() (string, error) {
630					err := m.client.DestroySandbox(id)
631					return fmt.Sprintf("%q destroyed", name), err
632				}))
633			default:
634				m.view = viewSandboxes
635				m.feedback = "cancelled"
636				m.feedErr = false
637			}
638		}
639		return m, nil
640	}
641	
642	// ---------- view ----------
643	
644	func (m tuiModel) View() string {
645		var b strings.Builder
646	
647		title := titleStyle.Render("  Sandcastle")
648		if m.client.ServerAlias != "" {
649			title += helpStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf("  %s", m.client.ServerAlias))
650		}
651		b.WriteString(title + "\n\n")
652	
653		switch m.view {
654		case viewSandboxes:
655			m.viewSandboxes(&b)
656		case viewRoutes:
657			m.viewRoutes(&b)
658		case viewCreateSandbox:
659			m.viewCreate(&b)
660		case viewAddRoute:
661			m.viewAddRoute(&b)
662		case viewConfirmDelete:
663			m.viewConfirmDelete(&b)
664		}
665	
666		// Feedback
667		if m.feedback != "" {
668			b.WriteString("\n")
669			if m.feedErr {
670				b.WriteString(errStyle.Render("  " + m.feedback))
671			} else {
672				b.WriteString(okStyle.Render("  " + m.feedback))
673			}
674			b.WriteString("\n")
675		}
676	
677		return b.String()
678	}
679	
680	func (m tuiModel) viewSandboxes(b *strings.Builder) {
681		if m.loading && len(m.sandboxes) == 0 {
682			b.WriteString("  " + m.spinner.View() + " Loading sandboxes...\n")
683			b.WriteString("\n" + helpStyle.Render("  q quit"))
684			return
685		}
686	
687		if len(m.sandboxes) == 0 {
688			b.WriteString("  No sandboxes. Press c to create one.\n")
689		} else {
690			// Header
691			b.WriteString(headerStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf("  %-22s %-10s %-18s %s", "NAME", "STATUS", "CREATED", "ROUTE")) + "\n")
692	
693			for i, sb := range m.sandboxes {
694				name := sb.Name
695				if sb.Temporary {
696					name += " ~"
697				}
698				if len(name) > 22 {
699					name = name[:21] + "…"
700				}
701	
702				st := statusStopped.Render(sb.Status)
703				if sb.Status == "running" {
704					st = statusRunning.Render(sb.Status)
705				}
706	
707				created := sb.CreatedAt.Local().Format("Jan 02 15:04")
708	
709				route := ""
710				if len(sb.Routes) > 0 {
711					parts := make([]string, 0, len(sb.Routes))
712					for _, r := range sb.Routes {
713						if r.Mode == "tcp" {
714							parts = append(parts, fmt.Sprintf("tcp/:%d", r.PublicPort))
715						} else {
716							parts = append(parts, r.Domain)
717						}
718					}
719					route = strings.Join(parts, ", ")
720					if len(route) > 30 {
721						route = route[:29] + "…"
722					}
723				}
724	
725				line := fmt.Sprintf("  %-22s %-10s %-18s %s", name, st, created, route)
726				if i == m.cursor {
727					// Re-render with selection styling — pad to width
728					padded := fmt.Sprintf("  %-22s %-20s %-18s %-30s", name, sb.Status, created, route)
729					line = selectedStyle.Render(padded)
730				}
731				b.WriteString(line + "\n")
732			}
733		}
734	
735		if m.loading {
736			b.WriteString("\n  " + m.spinner.View() + "\n")
737		}
738	
739		b.WriteString("\n")
740		b.WriteString(helpStyle.Render("  enter connect  c create  s start  x stop  d destroy  r routes  R refresh  q quit"))
741		b.WriteString("\n")
742	}
743	
744	func (m tuiModel) viewRoutes(b *strings.Builder) {
745		b.WriteString(headerStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf("  Routes for %s", m.routeSandbox.Name)) + "\n\n")
746	
747		if m.loading {
748			b.WriteString("  " + m.spinner.View() + " Loading routes...\n")
749			b.WriteString("\n" + helpStyle.Render("  esc back  q quit"))
750			return
751		}
752	
753		if len(m.routes) == 0 {
754			b.WriteString("  No routes. Press a to add one.\n")
755		} else {
756			b.WriteString(headerStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf("  %-6s %-6s %-30s %-8s %s", "ID", "MODE", "DOMAIN / PUBLIC PORT", "PORT", "URL")) + "\n")
757			for i, r := range m.routes {
758				target := r.Domain
759				if r.Mode == "tcp" {
760					target = fmt.Sprintf(":%d", r.PublicPort)
761				}
762				url := r.URL
763				if len(url) > 40 {
764					url = url[:39] + "…"
765				}
766				line := fmt.Sprintf("  %-6d %-6s %-30s %-8d %s", r.ID, r.Mode, target, r.Port, url)
767				if i == m.routeCursor {
768					line = selectedStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf("  %-6d %-6s %-30s %-8d %-40s", r.ID, r.Mode, target, r.Port, url))
769				}
770				b.WriteString(line + "\n")
771			}
772		}
773	
774		b.WriteString("\n")
775		b.WriteString(helpStyle.Render("  a add  d delete  R refresh  esc/q back"))
776		b.WriteString("\n")
777	}
778	
779	var (
780		formLabelStyle = lipgloss.NewStyle().
781				Width(14).
782				Foreground(lipgloss.Color("252"))
783	
784		formLabelActiveStyle = lipgloss.NewStyle().
785					Width(14).
786					Bold(true).
787					Foreground(lipgloss.Color("214"))
788	
789		checkOn  = lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color("42")).Render("[x]")
790		checkOff = lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color("245")).Render("[ ]")
791	)
792	
793	func (m tuiModel) viewCreate(b *strings.Builder) {
794		b.WriteString(headerStyle.Render("  Create Sandbox") + "\n\n")
795	
796		for i, f := range m.createFields {
797			active := i == m.createCursor
798			label := formLabelStyle.Render(f.label)
799			if active {
800				label = formLabelActiveStyle.Render(f.label)
801			}
802	
803			cursor := "  "
804			if active {
805				cursor = "> "
806			}
807	
808			switch f.kind {
809			case fieldText:
810				b.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s%s %s", cursor, label, f.input.View()))
811				// Show snapshot hints
812				if i == cfSnapshot && len(m.snapshots) > 0 && active {
813					current := strings.ToLower(f.input.Value())
814					if current == "" {
815						names := make([]string, 0, min(3, len(m.snapshots)))
816						for j, s := range m.snapshots {
817							if j >= 3 {
818								break
819							}
820							names = append(names, s.Name)
821						}
822						hint := strings.Join(names, ", ")
823						if len(m.snapshots) > 3 {
824							hint += fmt.Sprintf(" (+%d more)", len(m.snapshots)-3)
825						}
826						b.WriteString("  " + helpStyle.Render(hint))
827					}
828				}
829			case fieldBool:
830				check := checkOff
831				if f.boolVal {
832					check = checkOn
833				}
834				b.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s%s %s", cursor, label, check))
835			}
836			b.WriteString("\n")
837		}
838	
839		b.WriteString("\n")
840		b.WriteString(helpStyle.Render("  arrows/tab navigate  space toggle  ctrl+s create  esc cancel"))
841		b.WriteString("\n")
842	}
843	
844	func (m tuiModel) viewAddRoute(b *strings.Builder) {
845		b.WriteString(headerStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf("  Add Route to %s", m.routeSandbox.Name)) + "\n\n")
846		b.WriteString("  Domain: " + m.routeInputs[0].View() + "\n")
847		b.WriteString("  Port:   " + m.routeInputs[1].View() + "\n")
848		b.WriteString("\n")
849		b.WriteString(helpStyle.Render("  tab next field  enter confirm  esc cancel"))
850		b.WriteString("\n")
851	}
852	
853	func (m tuiModel) viewConfirmDelete(b *strings.Builder) {
854		b.WriteString(errStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf("  Destroy sandbox %q?", m.deleteTarget)) + "\n\n")
855		b.WriteString("  Press y to confirm, any other key to cancel.\n")
856	}
857	
858	// ---------- entry point ----------
859	
860	func runTUI() error {
861		client, err := api.NewClient()
862		if err != nil {
863			return err
864		}
865	
866		p := tea.NewProgram(newTUI(client), tea.WithAltScreen())
867		_, err = p.Run()
868		return err
869	}
870

The create dialog is now a full form with all sandbox options. Here's what the form looks like:

```
  Create Sandbox

> Name           [leave empty for auto-generated name        ]
  Image          [ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest  ]
  Snapshot       [snapshot name to restore from              ]
  Docker         [x]
  VNC            [x]
  Tailscale      [ ]
  Home           [ ]
  Data path      [subpath or . for root (empty = none)       ]
  SMB            [ ]
  Temporary      [ ]

  arrows/tab navigate  space toggle  ctrl+s create  esc cancel
```

**Fields:**
- **Name** - text, empty = auto-generated `temp-<timestamp>`
- **Image** - text, empty = default sandbox image
- **Snapshot** - text with tab-completion from API (right arrow autocompletes, shows hints when empty)
- **Docker** - toggle, default on
- **VNC** - toggle, default on
- **Tailscale** - toggle, default off
- **Home** - toggle (persistent home), default off
- **Data path** - text, path to mount at `/persisted`
- **SMB** - toggle, default off
- **Temporary** - toggle (auto-remove), default off

**Navigation:** up/down/tab to move between fields, space/enter to toggle bools, ctrl+s to submit from any field, enter on a text field also submits, esc to cancel.

136	// sourceLabel returns "env", "config", or "default" to annotate where a value comes from.
137	func sourceLabel(fromEnv, fromConfig bool) string {
138		if fromEnv {
139			return "env"
140		}
141		if fromConfig {
142			return "config"
143		}
144		return "default"
145	}

148		Use:   "set <key> <value>",
149		Short: "Set a CLI preference",
150		Long: `Set a CLI preference and save it to ~/.sandcastle/config.yaml.
151	
152	Valid keys:
153	  connect_protocol   Connection protocol: "ssh" (default) or "mosh"
154	  use_tmux           Wrap connection in tmux: "true" (default) or "false"
155	  ssh_extra_args     Extra flags appended to the ssh/mosh invocation
156	  mount_home         Mount persistent home on create: "true" or "false" (default)
157	  data_path          Mount user data dir on create: "." (root), subpath, or "off"
158	  vnc                Enable VNC on create: "true" (default) or "false"
159	  docker             Enable Docker (DinD) on create: "true" (default) or "false"
160	
161	ENV vars override config file values at runtime:
162	  SANDCASTLE_CONNECT_PROTOCOL, SANDCASTLE_USE_TMUX, SANDCASTLE_SSH_EXTRA_ARGS,
163	  SANDCASTLE_HOME, SANDCASTLE_DATA, SANDCASTLE_VNC, SANDCASTLE_DOCKER`,
164		Args: cobra.ExactArgs(2),
165		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
166			key, value := args[0], args[1]
167	
168			cfg, err := config.Load()
169			if err != nil {
170				return err
171			}
172	
173			if err := cfg.SetPreference(key, value); err != nil {

175			}

177			if err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {
178				return err
179			}
180

182			return nil
183		},
184	}

223	var serverUseCmd = &cobra.Command{
224		Use:   "use <alias>",
225		Short: "Set the active server",
226		Args:  cobra.ExactArgs(1),
227		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
228			alias := args[0]
229			cfg, err := config.Load()
230			if err != nil {
231				return err
232			}
233	
234			// Allow using URL as alias — find matching server
235			if _, ok := cfg.Servers[alias]; !ok {
236				for a, srv := range cfg.Servers {
237					if srv.URL == strings.TrimRight(alias, "/") {
238						alias = a
239						break
240					}
241				}
242			}
243	
244			if _, ok := cfg.Servers[alias]; !ok {
245				return fmt.Errorf("server %q not found — run: sandcastle server list", alias)
246			}
247	
248			cfg.CurrentServer = alias
249			if err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {
250				return err
251			}
252	
253			fmt.Printf("Switched to %s (%s)\n", alias, cfg.Servers[alias].URL)
254			return nil
255		},
256	}
257	
258	var serverRemoveCmd = &cobra.Command{
259		Use:     "remove <alias>",
260		Short:   "Remove a configured server",
261		Aliases: []string{"rm"},
262		Args:    cobra.ExactArgs(1),
263		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
264			alias := args[0]
265			cfg, err := config.Load()
266			if err != nil {
267				return err
268			}
269	
270			if _, ok := cfg.Servers[alias]; !ok {
271				return fmt.Errorf("server %q not found", alias)
272			}
273	
274			delete(cfg.Servers, alias)
275			if cfg.CurrentServer == alias {
276				cfg.CurrentServer = ""
277				// Set first remaining server as current
278				for a := range cfg.Servers {
279					cfg.CurrentServer = a
280					break
281				}
282			}
283	
284			if err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {
285				return err
286			}
287	
288			fmt.Printf("Removed server %s\n", alias)
289			return nil
290		},
291	}

1	package cmd
2	
3	import (
4		"bufio"
5		"fmt"
6		"net"
7		"net/url"
8		"os"
9		"os/exec"
10		"runtime"
11		"strings"
12		"time"
13	
14		"github.com/sandcastle/cli/api"
15		"github.com/sandcastle/cli/internal/config"
16		"github.com/spf13/cobra"
17	)
18	
19	func init() {
20		loginCmd.Flags().BoolP("insecure", "k", false, "Skip TLS certificate verification (for self-signed certs)")
21		rootCmd.AddCommand(loginCmd)
22	}
23	
24	var loginCmd = &cobra.Command{
25		Use:   "login <url> [alias]",
26		Short: "Authenticate with a Sandcastle server via browser",
27		Long: `Authenticate with a Sandcastle server using the device authorization flow.
28	Opens a browser window where you approve the CLI, then saves the token.
29	
30	Examples:
31	  sandcastle login https://demo.sandcastle.rocks
32	  sandcastle login https://demo.sandcastle.rocks prod
33	  sandcastle login http://localhost:3000 local`,
34		Args: cobra.RangeArgs(1, 2),
35		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
36			serverURL := strings.TrimRight(args[0], "/")
37			insecure, _ := cmd.Flags().GetBool("insecure")
38	
39			// Derive alias from URL if not provided, prompt interactively
40			alias := ""
41			if len(args) > 1 {
42				alias = args[1]
43			} else {
44				suggested := deriveAlias(serverURL)
45				fmt.Printf("Server alias [%s]: ", suggested)
46				reader := bufio.NewReader(os.Stdin)
47				input, _ := reader.ReadString('\n')
48				input = strings.TrimSpace(input)
49				if input != "" {
50					alias = input
51				} else {
52					alias = suggested
53				}
54			}
55	
56			// Get device code from server
57			hostname, _ := os.Hostname()
58			client := api.NewClientWithToken(serverURL, "", insecure)
59			deviceCode, err := client.RequestDeviceCode(fmt.Sprintf("cli-%s", hostname))
60			if err != nil {
61				return fmt.Errorf("requesting device code: %w", err)
62			}
63	
64			fmt.Printf("Your code: %s\n\n", deviceCode.UserCode)
65			fmt.Printf("Open this URL to authorize:\n  %s\n\n", deviceCode.VerificationURL)
66	
67			// Try to open browser
68			if err := openBrowser(deviceCode.VerificationURL); err == nil {
69				fmt.Println("Browser opened. Waiting for authorization...")
70			} else {
71				fmt.Println("Waiting for authorization...")
72			}
73	
74			// Poll for token
75			interval := time.Duration(deviceCode.Interval) * time.Second
76			if interval < time.Second {
77				interval = 3 * time.Second
78			}
79	
80			deadline := time.Now().Add(time.Duration(deviceCode.ExpiresIn) * time.Second)
81			for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
82				time.Sleep(interval)
83	
84				token, pending, err := client.PollDeviceToken(deviceCode.DeviceCode)
85				if err != nil {
86					return fmt.Errorf("authorization failed: %w", err)
87				}
88				if pending {
89					continue
90				}
91	
92				// Save to config
93				cfg, err := config.Load()
94				if err != nil {
95					return fmt.Errorf("loading config: %w", err)
96				}
97	
98				cfg.SetServer(alias, serverURL, token, insecure)
99				if err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {
100					return fmt.Errorf("saving config: %w", err)
101				}
102	
103				fmt.Printf("\nLogged in to %s (alias: %s)\n", serverURL, alias)
104				return nil
105			}
106	
107			return fmt.Errorf("authorization timed out — please try again")
108		},
109	}
110	
111	func deriveAlias(serverURL string) string {
112		u, err := url.Parse(serverURL)
113		if err != nil {
114			return "default"
115		}
116		host := u.Hostname()
117	
118		// Use "local" for localhost
119		if host == "localhost" || host == "127.0.0.1" {
120			return "local"
121		}
122	
123		// For IP addresses, use the full IP as suggestion
124		if net.ParseIP(host) != nil {
125			return host
126		}
127	
128		// For domains, use the first subdomain
129		parts := strings.Split(host, ".")
130		if len(parts) > 0 {
131			return parts[0]
132		}
133		return "default"
134	}
135	
136	func openBrowser(url string) error {
137		switch runtime.GOOS {
138		case "darwin":
139			return exec.Command("open", url).Start()
140		case "linux":
141			return exec.Command("xdg-open", url).Start()
142		case "windows":
143			return exec.Command("rundll32", "url.dll,FileProtocolHandler", url).Start()
144		default:
145			return fmt.Errorf("unsupported platform")
146		}
147	}
148

toggle\n\t\tif m.addServerFocus == 2 && msg.String() == \" \" {\n\t\t\tm.addServerInsecure = !m.addServerInsecure\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tif m.addServerFocus < 2 {\n\t\tvar cmd tea.Cmd\n\t\tm.addServerInputs[m.addServerFocus], cmd = m.addServerInputs[m.addServerFocus].Update(msg)\n\t\treturn m, cmd\n\t}\n\treturn m, nil\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) submitAddServer() (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tserverURL := strings.TrimSpace(m.addServerInputs[0].Value())\n\tif serverURL == \"\" {\n\t\tm.feedback = \"URL is required\"\n\t\tm.feedErr = true\n\t\treturn m, nil\n\t}\n\tserverURL = strings.TrimRight(serverURL, \"/\")\n\n\talias := strings.TrimSpace(m.addServerInputs[1].Value())\n\tif alias == \"\" {\n\t\talias = tuiDeriveAlias(serverURL)\n\t}\n\n\tm.loginAlias = alias\n\tm.loginURL = serverURL\n\tm.loginInsecure = m.addServerInsecure\n\tm.loginClient = api.NewClientWithToken(serverURL, \"\", m.addServerInsecure)\n\tm.view = viewServerLogin\n\tm.loginPolling = false\n\tm.loginUserCode = \"\"\n\tm.loginVerifyURL = \"\"\n\tm.feedback = \"\"\n\n\treturn m, requestDeviceCode(m.loginClient)\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) updateServerLogin(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, viewServers\n\t\t\tm.feedback = \"Login cancelled\"\n\t\t\tm.feedErr = false\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn m, nil\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) updateConfirmRemoveServer(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n\tcase tea.KeyMsg:\n\t\tswitch {\n\t\tcase key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys(\"y\", \"Y\"))):\n\t\t\talias := m.removeServerAlias\n\t\t\tcfg, err := config.Load()\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tm.feedback = err.Error()\n\t\t\t\tm.feedErr = true\n\t\t\t\tm.view = viewServers\n\t\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tdelete(cfg.Servers, alias)\n\t\t\tif cfg.CurrentServer == alias {\n\t\t\t\tcfg.CurrentServer = \"\"\n\t\t\t\tfor a := range cfg.Servers {\n\t\t\t\t\tcfg.CurrentServer = a\n\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tm.feedback = err.Error()\n\t\t\t\tm.feedErr = true\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tm.feedback = fmt.Sprintf(\"Removed %s\", alias)\n\t\t\t\tm.feedErr = false\n\t\t\t\t// Recreate client if server changed\n\t\t\t\tif newClient, err := api.NewClient(); err == nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tm.client = newClient\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tm.servers = loadServerList()\n\t\t\tif m.serverCursor >= len(m.servers) {\n\t\t\t\tm.serverCursor = max(0, len(m.servers)-1)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tm.view = viewServers\n\t\t\treturn m, nil\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\tm.view = viewServers\n\t\t\tm.feedback = \"cancelled\"\n\t\t\tm.feedErr = false\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn m, nil\n}\n\n// ---------- server views ----------\n\nvar (\n\tserverActiveStyle = lipgloss.NewStyle().\n\t\t\t\tBold(true).\n\t\t\t\tForeground(lipgloss.Color(\"42\"))\n\n\tserverAuthStyle = lipgloss.NewStyle().\n\t\t\tForeground(lipgloss.Color(\"245\"))\n\n\tcodeStyle = lipgloss.NewStyle().\n\t\t\tBold(true).\n\t\t\tForeground(lipgloss.Color(\"229\")).\n\t\t\tBackground(lipgloss.Color(\"57\")).\n\t\t\tPadding(0, 1)\n)\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) viewServers(b *strings.Builder) {\n\tb.WriteString(headerStyle.Render(\"  Servers\") + \"\\n\\n\")\n\n\tif len(m.servers) == 0 {\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"  No servers configured. Press a to add one.\\n\")\n\t} else {\n\t\tb.WriteString(headerStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\"  %-3s %-16s %-40s %s\", \"\", \"ALIAS\", \"URL\", \"STATUS\")) + \"\\n\")\n\t\tfor i, s := range m.servers {\n\t\t\tmarker := \"   \"\n\t\t\tif s.active {\n\t\t\t\tmarker = serverActiveStyle.Render(\" * \")\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tstatus := serverAuthStyle.Render(\"no token\")\n\t\t\tif s.hasToken {\n\t\t\t\tstatus = okStyle.Render(\"authenticated\")\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\talias := s.alias\n\t\t\tif len(alias) > 16 {\n\t\t\t\talias = alias[:15] + \"…\"\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tsurl := s.url\n\t\t\tif len(surl) > 40 {\n\t\t\t\tsurl = surl[:39] + \"…\"\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\t\tline := fmt.Sprintf(\"%s %-16s %-40s %s\", marker, alias, surl, status)\n\t\t\tif i == m.serverCursor {\n\t\t\t\tline = selectedStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\"%s %-16s %-40s %-15s\", marker, alias, surl, func() string {\n\t\t\t\t\tif s.hasToken {\n\t\t\t\t\t\treturn \"authenticated\"\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t\treturn \"no token\"\n\t\t\t\t}()))\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tb.WriteString(line + \"\\n\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n\tb.WriteString(helpStyle.Render(\"  enter select  a add  d remove  esc back\"))\n\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) viewAddServer(b *strings.Builder) {\n\tb.WriteString(headerStyle.Render(\"  Add Server\") + \"\\n\\n\")\n\n\tlabels := [3]string{\"URL\", \"Alias\", \"Insecure\"}\n\tfor i := 0; i < 3; i++ {\n\t\tactive := i == m.addServerFocus\n\t\tlabel := formLabelStyle.Render(labels[i])\n\t\tif active {\n\t\t\tlabel = formLabelActiveStyle.Render(labels[i])\n\t\t}\n\t\tcursor := \"  \"\n\t\tif active {\n\t\t\tcursor = \"> \"\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif i < 2 {\n\t\t\tb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"%s%s %s\\n\", cursor, label, m.addServerInputs[i].View()))\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tcheck := checkOff\n\t\t\tif m.addServerInsecure {\n\t\t\t\tcheck = checkOn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tb.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf(\"%s%s %s  %s\\n\", cursor, label, check, helpStyle.Render(\"skip TLS verification\")))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n\tb.WriteString(helpStyle.Render(\"  tab navigate  space toggle  ctrl+s/enter submit  esc cancel\"))\n\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) viewServerLogin(b *strings.Builder) {\n\tb.WriteString(headerStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\"  Login to %s\", m.loginAlias)) + \"\\n\\n\")\n\n\tif m.loginUserCode == \"\" {\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"  \" + m.spinner.View() + \" Requesting device code...\\n\")\n\t} else {\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"  Your code:  \" + codeStyle.Render(m.loginUserCode) + \"\\n\\n\")\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"  Authorize at:\\n\")\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"  \" + headerStyle.Render(m.loginVerifyURL) + \"\\n\\n\")\n\t\tb.WriteString(\"  \" + m.spinner.View() + \" Waiting for authorization...\\n\")\n\t}\n\n\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n\tb.WriteString(helpStyle.Render(\"  esc cancel\"))\n\tb.WriteString(\"\\n\")\n}\n\nfunc (m tuiModel) viewConfirmRemoveServer(b *strings.Builder) {\n\tb.WriteString(errStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\"  Remove server %q?\", m.removeServerAlias)) + \"\\n\\n\")\n\tb.WriteString(\"  Press y to confirm, any other key to cancel.\\n\")\n}\n\n// ---------- entry point ----------"

> AGENT

Everything looks good. The file is 1406 lines and builds clean. Here's what was added:

**New views:**

| View | Key | Description |
|------|-----|-------------|
| **Servers** | `S` from sandbox list | Lists all configured servers with alias, URL, auth status, and active marker (`*`) |
| **Add Server** | `a` from servers view | Form with URL, alias (auto-derived if empty), and insecure toggle |
| **Server Login** | auto after add | Shows device code + verification URL, opens browser, polls for auth |
| **Remove Server** | `d` from servers view | y/n confirmation, auto-selects next server if active one is removed |

**Keybindings (servers view):**
- `j/k` or arrows -- navigate
- `enter` -- switch to selected server (reloads client + sandbox list)
- `a` -- add new server (device auth flow)
- `d` -- remove selected server
- `esc/q` -- back to sandbox list (reloads sandboxes for potentially new server)

**Device auth flow:**
1. Enter URL + optional alias + optional insecure toggle
2. Requests device code from server
3. Shows the code prominently and opens browser automatically
4. Polls every 3 seconds for authorization
5. On success, saves token to config and switches to the new server
6. `esc` cancels at any point

122	// Priority: ENV var > config file preference > built-in default.
123	func (c *Config) LoadPreferences() Preferences {
124		p := c.Preferences
125	
126		if v := os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_CONNECT_PROTOCOL"); v != "" {
127			p.ConnectProtocol = v
128		}
129		if v := os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_USE_TMUX"); v != "" {
130			b := strings.ToLower(v) == "true" || v == "1"
131			p.UseTmux = &b
132		}
133		if v := os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_SSH_EXTRA_ARGS"); v != "" {
134			p.SSHExtraArgs = v
135		}
136		if v := os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_HOME"); v != "" {
137			b := strings.ToLower(v) == "true" || v == "1"
138			p.MountHome = &b
139		}
140		if v := os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_DATA"); v != "" {
141			if v == "1" || v == "true" {
142				v = "."
143			}
144			p.DataPath = v
145		}
146		if v := os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_VNC"); v != "" {
147			b := strings.ToLower(v) == "true" || v == "1"
148			p.VNC = &b
149		}
150		if v := os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_DOCKER"); v != "" {
151			b := strings.ToLower(v) == "true" || v == "1"
152			p.Docker = &b
153		}
154	
155		// Apply built-in defaults
156		if p.ConnectProtocol == "" {
157			p.ConnectProtocol = "ssh"
158		}
159		if p.UseTmux == nil {
160			t := true
161			p.UseTmux = &t
162		}
163	
164		return p
165	}
166	
167	// SetPreference sets a named preference by string value and validates it.
168	func (c *Config) SetPreference(key, value string) error {
169		switch key {
170		case "connect_protocol":
171			if value != "ssh" && value != "mosh" && value != "auto" {
172				return fmt.Errorf("connect_protocol must be 'ssh', 'mosh', or 'auto', got %q", value)
173			}
174			if value == "auto" {
175				c.Preferences.ConnectProtocol = ""
176			} else {
177				c.Preferences.ConnectProtocol = value
178			}
179		case "use_tmux":
180			switch strings.ToLower(value) {
181			case "true", "1", "yes":
182				t := true
183				c.Preferences.UseTmux = &t
184			case "false", "0", "no":
185				f := false
186				c.Preferences.UseTmux = &f
187			default:
188				return fmt.Errorf("use_tmux must be 'true' or 'false', got %q", value)
189			}
190		case "ssh_extra_args":
191			c.Preferences.SSHExtraArgs = value
192		case "mount_home":
193			switch strings.ToLower(value) {
194			case "true", "1", "yes":
195				t := true
196				c.Preferences.MountHome = &t
197			case "false", "0", "no":
198				f := false
199				c.Preferences.MountHome = &f
200			default:
201				return fmt.Errorf("mount_home must be 'true' or 'false', got %q", value)
202			}
203		case "data_path":
204			if value == "false" || value == "0" || value == "no" || value == "off" {
205				c.Preferences.DataPath = ""
206			} else {
207				if value == "true" || value == "1" {
208					value = "."
209				}
210				c.Preferences.DataPath = value
211			}
212		case "vnc":
213			switch strings.ToLower(value) {
214			case "true", "1", "yes":
215				t := true
216				c.Preferences.VNC = &t
217			case "false", "0", "no":
218				f := false
219				c.Preferences.VNC = &f
220			default:
221				return fmt.Errorf("vnc must be 'true' or 'false', got %q", value)
222			}
223		case "docker":
224			switch strings.ToLower(value) {
225			case "true", "1", "yes":
226				t := true
227				c.Preferences.Docker = &t
228			case "false", "0", "no":
229				f := false
230				c.Preferences.Docker = &f
231			default:
232				return fmt.Errorf("docker must be 'true' or 'false', got %q", value)
233			}
234		default:
235			return fmt.Errorf("unknown preference %q; valid keys: connect_protocol, use_tmux, ssh_extra_args, mount_home, data_path, vnc, docker", key)
236		}
237		return nil
238	}

423	func (m tuiModel) updateSandboxes(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
424		switch msg := msg.(type) {
425		case tea.KeyMsg:
426			m.feedback = ""
427			switch {
428			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("q", "ctrl+c"))):
429				return m, tea.Quit
430			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("up", "k"))):
431				if m.cursor > 0 {
432					m.cursor--
433				}
434			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("down", "j"))):
435				if m.cursor < len(m.sandboxes)-1 {
436					m.cursor++
437				}
438			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("c"))):
439				m.view = viewCreateSandbox
440				m.createFields = buildCreateFields()
441				m.createCursor = 0
442				m.createFields[0].input.Focus()
443				cmds := []tea.Cmd{m.createFields[0].input.Cursor.BlinkCmd()}
444				if len(m.snapshots) == 0 {
445					cmds = append(cmds, loadSnapshots(m.client))
446				}
447				return m, tea.Batch(cmds...)
448			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("r"))):
449				if len(m.sandboxes) > 0 {
450					m.loading = true
451					m.feedback = ""
452					sb := m.sandboxes[m.cursor]
453					m.routeSandbox = &sb
454					m.view = viewRoutes
455					return m, tea.Batch(m.spinner.Tick, loadRoutes(m.client, sb.ID))
456				}
457			case key.Matches(msg, key.NewBinding(key.WithKeys("R"))):
458				m.loading = true
459				m.feedback = ""

348				m.view = viewServers
349				return m, nil
350			}
351			if msg.pending {
352				// Keep polling
353				return m, pollDeviceToken(m.loginClient, m.loginDeviceCode.DeviceCode)
354			}
355			// Success — save token and switch to the new server
356			cfg, err := config.Load()
357			if err != nil {
358				m.feedback = err.Error()
359				m.feedErr = true
360			} else {
361				cfg.SetServer(m.loginAlias, m.loginURL, msg.token, m.loginInsecure)
362				if err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {
363					m.feedback = err.Error()
364					m.feedErr = true
365				} else {
366					m.feedback = fmt.Sprintf("Logged in to %s", m.loginAlias)
367					m.feedErr = false
368					// Recreate client for the new server
369					newClient, clientErr := api.NewClient()
370					if clientErr == nil {
371						m.client = newClient
372					}
373				}
374			}
375			m.loginPolling = false
376			m.view = viewServers
377			m.servers = loadServerList()
378			return m, nil
379	
380		case actionDoneMsg:
381			m.loading = false
382			if msg.err != nil {
383				m.feedback = msg.err.Error()
384				m.feedErr = true
```

5. **Data path** — text
6. **VNC** — bool (default true)

233			client:       client,
234			spinner:      s,
235			loading:      true,
236			createFields: buildCreateFields(),
237			routeInputs:  [2]textinput.Model{domainInput, portInput},
238		}
239	}

}

> AGENT

Done. Here's what was added:

**Settings screen** (`P` from sandbox list) — edits `~/.sandcastle/config.yaml` preferences:

```
  Preferences
  Defaults for new sandboxes and connections

> Protocol         ssh   mosh   auto
  Tmux            [x]
  SSH extra args  [                                             ]
  Mount home      [ ]
  Data path       [                                             ]
  VNC             [x]
  Docker          [x]

  arrows/tab navigate  space/enter toggle  left/right cycle  ctrl+s save  esc cancel
```

**Fields:**
| Field | Type | Description |
|-------|------|-------------|
| **Protocol** | cycle (ssh/mosh/auto) | Connection protocol, left/right or space to cycle |
| **Tmux** | toggle | Wrap connection in tmux session |
| **SSH extra args** | text | Extra flags appended to ssh/mosh invocation |
| **Mount home** | toggle | Mount persistent home on create |
| **Data path** | text | Data directory to mount at /persisted |
| **VNC** | toggle | Enable VNC on create |
| **Docker** | toggle | Enable Docker (DinD) on create |

The form loads current values from config (respecting env var overrides for display), and `ctrl+s` saves all fields back to `~/.sandcastle/config.yaml`. The cycle field uses a highlighted pill-style selector for the current option.
